# Decisions

## Never marketing price for One's staff messages, and thirteen copies reworded — 12 November 2026

Janmejay, 12 Nov 2026, asked whether staff messages Meta had filed as
marketing should still go out: "Reword them".

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-581 | **One never sends a template Meta has filed as marketing, unless the owner allows it (`runtime_flags.whatsapp_allow_marketing`, missing = no). Such a message stays inside One, with the plain reason "filed as marketing", and Settings → WhatsApp marks the template "Filed as marketing: not sent" and leaves it out of the feature's ready count. The thirteen staff copies Meta re-filed or turned down are reworded under `_v2` names (a per-language `version` in `staff-hub-templates.ts`), and the hourly check sends them to Meta** | Of the 40 staff and teacher-task copies sent on 12 Nov (D-573), Meta approved them as UTILITY and then re-filed 12 as MARKETING within minutes (the hub's webhook log, `template_category_update`): both copies of the staff notice, the system check, the reversal or write-off request and the several-messages digest, and the Hindi copies of cover cancelled, weekly summary, reversal decided and new task. It turned down the Hindi online-payment check as `INCORRECT_CATEGORY`. Marketing costs about 7 times as much (the `_v8` note in `campaigns.ts`) and Meta holds marketing back from people who get many, so a cover or task alert could silently not arrive. Meta's call differed between the Hindi and English copies of the same message, so rewording is worth a try: no "open the app" lines, each says whose work it is. Meta never takes a name twice, so the `_v1` rows stay as history and are never sent. Registry rows `20261112064013_whatsapp_staff_hub_reworded.sql` (13, `not_submitted`); `tests/whatsapp-platform.test.ts` (the guard) and `tests/whatsapp-staff-hub-templates.test.ts` (the `_v2` set) |

## Veer Patta AI, step 2: a hand-off reaches a person, and the answer comes back — 12 November 2026

Janmejay, 12 Nov 2026: complaints go to the class teacher first, then to an
admin or the principal; fee complaints to the accountant and an admin; staff
are told only from 6 am to 9 pm. Step 1's live test on his number passed the
same morning (six messages, six answers, no double answer). Step 2 stays
practice only: practice mode on, the `parent_helper` switch off, real
recipients off.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-578 | **A hand-off now reaches its person on WhatsApp, between 6 am and 9 pm India time only, and keeps going until someone answers.** The child's class teacher for a message to the teacher; the accountant and an admin for fees; an admin for the principal; the office (else an admin) for the office; at most three people, oldest account first, always of the session's own kind (D-560). They are told at once in the day, at 6 am for a message that came at night. Unanswered 3 hours after the alert: one reminder. A complaint (to the principal, or about a complaint) for the class teacher or the office, still unanswered a day after the first alert, goes up to an admin, who gets one reminder of their own; anything else gets a second reminder at a day, then stops. A complaint whose class teacher cannot be reached goes up at once; anything else nobody can be told about stays in Messages. An answer, or the message marked done in Messages, stops it all. Practice (the owner's test number): only the first person is told, on the owner's own number, and the alert says whose place it stands in | The owner's answers of 12 Nov. The job is pg_cron `parent-helper`, every five minutes and only when a step is due, calling `POST /api/jobs/parent-helper` with the WhatsApp queue's Vault secret, as `whatsapp-queue` does; `parent_helper_take_due` holds each step for ten minutes so two runs never act on it twice, and a staff list that cannot be read is tried again in 15 minutes. Every alert passes `checkWhatsAppSend` (feature `parent_helper`): practice mode or the switch off stops the hand-off quietly and it stays in Messages. An alert is typed while the person's chat with the school is open (they wrote within 23.5 hours), else it is the approved template, and the template too when Meta answers that the chat closed after all. Each alert is written down with its message id (`parent_helper_alerts`), and a person with no WhatsApp number is noted, never skipped silently. Migration `20261112052103_parent_helper_handoffs.sql` (applied to One 12 Nov 2026, service-role only, quarantine K). `domain/helper-handoff.ts`, `data/helper-handoffs.ts`, `data/helper-relay.ts`; `tests/whatsapp-parent-helper-handoff.test.ts`, `tests/whatsapp-parent-helper-relay.test.ts` |
| D-579 | **A person answers by swiping right on the alert and typing; the answer is saved in Messages and goes to the parent from the school's number.** A reply counts as an answer only when it quotes one of the helper's alerts and comes from the number that alert went to; it is answered once however often the hub forwards it, and the person is thanked, or asked to type when they sent a voice note or a photo. An answer typed in One's Messages to a message the helper passed on goes the same way. While the parent's chat is open the answer goes as it is, under who answered (the class teacher by name, else accounts, the principal's office or the school office) and the parent's own words; otherwise the parent gets the approved "your answer is ready" note and the answer waits (`parent_helper_deliveries`) until they next write, when it goes before anything else, and a bare "ok, thanks" then needs no other reply. The message is marked done in Messages once the answer is on its way, and left open when it could not go | Meta lets a business message someone freely only within 24 hours of their last message, so a parent who has gone quiet can only be sent an approved template. Answers that wait are taken before they are sent, so two messages in a row never send one twice. The answer's words stay in `parent_reply_answers`, where every answer from Messages already goes. While the helper may not message the parent (switched off, practice mode, real recipients off) Messages answers it the old way, through its own queue, so an answer always has a way out. `handleStaffAnswer`, `deliverWaitingAnswers`, `relayFromMessages` in `data/helper-relay.ts`; `isAcknowledgement` in `domain/helper-handoff.ts`; `tests/whatsapp-parent-helper-turn.test.ts` |
| D-580 | **Four new templates on the school's hub, UTILITY, no buttons: the staff alert (`vpps_staff_parent_question_{hi,en}_v1`: name, student, about, message) and the parent's note (`vpps_parent_answer_ready_{hi,en}_v1`: who answered, about whom).** Registry rows `not_submitted`, feature `parent_helper`; the hourly template check sends them to Meta by itself (D-573). Until they are approved a person is told only while their chat with the school is open; the rest is noted and the reminders still come | A staff member's language picks the alert's (`both` reads Hindi); the parent's note follows the parent's (Hinglish reads Hindi). The note names the child, or the topic in plain words when no child was named. Submitting a template sends nothing to anyone. `domain/helper-hub-templates.ts`; `tests/whatsapp-staff-hub-templates.test.ts` and `tests/whatsapp-parent-helper-handoff.test.ts` check the bodies against Meta's shape rules and the registry rows |

## Veer Patta AI, the school's WhatsApp helper — 12 November 2026

Janmejay, 12 Nov 2026: a helper on the school's WhatsApp number for parents and
teachers, answering day and night, "fast and careful when needed"; complaints go
to the class teacher first, then to an admin or the principal; fee complaints to
the accountant and an admin; staff are alerted only from 6 am to 9 pm; its name
is "Veer Patta AI". Step 1 is practice only: the owner's test number, standing
for two TEST children.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-574 | **Veer Patta AI answers from One, and only after the hub hands the message over (`POST /v1/typing`, hub build `2026-11-12.helper-claims`).** The hub forwards each message a parent writes; One reads the family's records, decides, claims the message, then answers. Every check before the claim may give up, and then the hub's own bot answers as before; once a message is claimed One always answers (Claude's words, a card or the menu). Step 1 knows one number: the owner's test number, standing for the practice children in `parent_helper_settings` (two TEST students). The `parent_helper` switch, practice mode and the owner-only rule all apply through `checkWhatsAppSend` | One and the hub's bot must never both answer: the hub's claim table (`app_claims`, insert-or-ignore) decides, and the hub says no when a person in the office is handling the chat or the message is more than 10 minutes old. The helper starts at once beside the webhook's own work (`alongside`), because the hub's bot waits only 5 seconds. A repeated delivery is never answered twice (the ledger row's unique message id). Only the hub's webhook starts it: a message straight from Meta has nobody to claim it from. `src/modules/whatsapp/data/helper-turn.ts`, `tests/whatsapp-parent-helper-turn.test.ts` |
| D-575 | **Figures come only from the school's records, as cards; Claude chooses the words and which card.** A tap on the helper's own buttons, or a short plain ask ("fees", "हाज़िरी", "receipt bhejo"), is answered from its card at once. Anything else goes to Claude: Haiku 5.5 first, Opus 5.5 when Haiku marks the message as needing care (the owner's "fast and careful when needed"). Anthropic only: the free models One uses elsewhere never see a child's record. Claude is asked only while One's AI helper is switched on and this month's AI spend is under its budget (the helper's cost counts against it); otherwise the parent gets the menu and the office | Claude is told never to write an amount, a mark or an attendance figure, and to name the card instead (`show`); the card under its words is built from `vppsone.parent_helper_child`, the helper's one read, with fees and the late fee kept apart (the Fees rule) and a write-off never shown as a receipt. Hindi, Hinglish and English, in forms that do not guess anyone's gender. `src/modules/whatsapp/domain/helper-*.ts`, `tests/whatsapp-parent-helper.test.ts` |
| D-576 | **What a parent writes is not kept in One.** Per number, One keeps only the language, what the helper is waiting for (up to 30 minutes) and when it last greeted (`parent_helper_chats`); per message, only how it was answered: route, intent, model, cost, time taken, any hand-off and the reply's id (`parent_helper_turns`). The hub keeps the conversation, as it keeps every chat on the school's number. The helper's replies are not written to `whatsapp_messages` | A hand-off is the one exception: the parent's message put in front of the school on purpose (`parent_replies`, Messages). Writing the replies to `whatsapp_messages` would have made `isReplyToOne` treat everything the parent wrote for the next seven days as a reply to One and copy it into `whatsapp_inbound`. Logs carry masked numbers and never the words. At most 40 answers per number a day; "stop" is never answered (the hub keeps the opt-out list). Migration `20261112041158_parent_helper.sql`, service-role only |
| D-577 | **A hand-off goes into One's Messages inbox, to one person: the child's class teacher for a message to the teacher, an accountant (else an admin) for fees, an admin for the principal, and nobody in particular for the office; always someone of the child's own kind (D-560).** The parent is told who it went to and, between 9 pm and 6 am India time, that it will be seen after 6 am. If the hand-off cannot be saved, the parent is told so, with the office's number | Step 1 stops at the inbox. Next, with the templates they need: the staff alert on WhatsApp (6 am to 9 pm), the staff member's answer going back to the parent, and a complaint the class teacher cannot settle moving to an admin or the principal |

## One and Fees apart on WhatsApp, and One's own templates on the hub — 11 and 12 November 2026

Janmejay, 11 Nov 2026: "Yes, fix them ... keep both the apps separate and sending
the message from both the apps should be different now." The Fees app keeps its
own AiSensy number for now (it retires in a few months), so no number is
hard-wired. Then, 12 Nov, asked whether One's own messages should get templates
on the school's number: "Yes submit them".

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-573 | **One's own messages get templates on the school's hub: the parents' school notices as they are (absent today, school closed, exam schedule, PTM; Hindi and English, Call office), and a hub copy of every staff notice and teacher task, one name per message and language (`vpps_staff_<slug>_<hi\|en>_v1`). One sends a staff notice or task under its hub name at the last step (`staffHubKey`): the reader's language for a staff notice (`both` reads Hindi, as the renderers fill it), the code's own language for a task, and for a free-text notice with no reader the words (any Devanagari is Hindi). The hourly template check also sends any of One's templates that has never been sent for approval, up to 15 an hour; one the hub refused or Meta rejected waits for an admin's Send for approval (24 a press)** | The staff templates were AiSensy's: one English body per name, most starting with the name (`{{1}}, …`) and ending with a link (`… {{6}}`). Meta refuses a body that starts or ends with a blank, and a Hindi reader would have had English words around Hindi values. The hub copies keep every blank in its old order, so `notices.ts` and the task delivery fill them unchanged; each gets a bold title, the school's name, a labelled line per value and a closing line, like the approved `_v8` set. `src/modules/whatsapp/domain/staff-hub-templates.ts`; registry rows `20261112024037_whatsapp_staff_hub_templates.sql` (40 rows, `not_submitted`); `tests/whatsapp-staff-hub-templates.test.ts` checks every body against Meta's shape rules (no blank first or last, none side by side, numbered in order, an example each) and every row against the catalogue. Not included: the free-text parent messages (`vx_school_notice`, `vppsone_parent_notice`), which Meta files as MARKETING at several times the price, and the teacher collection link. Settings → WhatsApp's hub card now covers the whole list ("Messages on the school's number"). Nothing reaches anyone: practice mode, every feature switch off and real recipients off are unchanged |
| D-572 | **One checks the hub's templates by itself once an hour (`POST /api/jobs/whatsapp-templates`, pg_cron job `whatsapp-templates` at minute 17, the queue's Vault secret)** | So a template never sits as "waiting" for days when a webhook event was missed. Migration `20261111060151`; first run 11 Nov 06:17 UTC, 22 approved |
| D-571 | **One keeps only what is its own from the hub: a reply to One (it quotes a message One sent, or comes within 7 days after One messaged that number), and statuses only for messages One sent** | The hub forwards everything it receives on the school's number; the rest belongs to the hub's own inbox. `isReplyToOne` in `platform/whatsapp/store.ts`; `whatsapp_record_status` writes nothing for a message One never sent (`20261111060151`) |
| D-570 | **The Fees app sends fee reminders, receipts, reversal notices and fee statements to parents, from its own number; One refuses them while `runtime_flags.whatsapp_fee_sender` is `fees` (a missing row reads as `fees`). Only `one`, on the owner's word once the Fees app has stopped, lets One send them** | No family gets the same reminder twice from two numbers. `FEES_APP_FEATURES` and `sentByFeesApp` in `platform/whatsapp/policy.ts`; Settings → WhatsApp shows "Fees app" on those four rows. PR 347. The Fees app can later join the hub with its own key |

## APInex's free models in the AI helper — 10 November 2026

Janmejay, 10 Nov 2026: "Also deploy the free api in the one app smartly the
models which is best for each work." The key is APInex's (`sk-apx-…`), kept in
his vault as `FREE_API_KEY`, with a zero balance, so only its `free/…` models.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-569 | **APInex's free models wait for the account holder's daily check-in, and One treats that as the whole account resting, never as a verdict on the models.** The check from production at 11:34 UTC on 10 Nov, the first after D-568, read the catalogue (nine models recorded without a call: eight billed, one decision model) and asked the other twelve; every one answered 402 "Daily check-in required to use free models. Please visit https://apinex.bond/overview to check in." `apinexRefusal` names it `check_in`; at draft time it rests all of APInex for half an hour like no credit (`billing`), so a check-in done later lifts it by itself; in the check it asks no plan, and `pickApinexModels` treats it (and the day's allowance) as not checked, so the jobs keep their usual models instead of losing APInex for three days. The Free models card says in plain words that APInex lends its free models only after the account holder checks in on apinex.bond that day and that the other free AIs write until then; the check's answer says the same. A check whose answer is lost on a slow connection still finishes and saves on the server, and the card looks again at 30, 70 and 130 s. | APInex's page sends the check-in as `POST /api/user/checkin` with a Cloudflare Turnstile token, a human check: One never tries to do it, and doing it each day is the owner's call. The open-source gateway (`Apinex-AI/APInexGate`, 1 Nov) has no check-in at all, so this is newer than its code. Tests: `tests/assist-apinex.test.ts` (the refusal, a check that met only the check-in, the jobs keeping their models). |
| D-568 | **Not every `free/…` model APInex lists is free, and its free account allows 15 requests a minute; One now reads APInex's refusals and paces its check.** The first check from production (10 Nov, 10:57 UTC) found the firewall lets Vercel in and 21 free models listed, but no model answered: about 17 calls came back 402 and the rest 429. APInex's own gateway (open source, `Apinex-AI/APInexGate`) bills a listed `free/…` model that is not on its Free provider from the wallet ("Insufficient balance" at $0), says "Free daily limit reached" when the day's free tokens are spent, and allows a free account 15 requests a minute for the whole account. So: `chatDraft` now carries the provider's own short words (`said`, key-shaped text removed, 140 characters) and a 429's Retry-After on its error; `apinexRefusal` sorts a 402 into "not free on this account" (that model only, rested like a missing model) or the day's allowance (all of APInex), and a 429's per-minute limit now rests all of APInex. `failureOf` takes a known problem a caller already worked out. Before it asks anything, the check reads APInex's public catalogue (`/api/public/models`, no key sent): a `free/…` model counts as free only when it is listed with `allowFree: true` and without the `decision` tag, the same rule as the vault; the others are recorded as "not free on this account" or "not a writing model" without a call and are not counted, and when the catalogue cannot be read or names none of the listed models every model is asked and its refusal read instead. The check starts probes about 4 s apart, three at most at once, honours Retry-After once, asks every model's notice first and a model's plan only if it could answer at all, carries a "not free" finding over for a week without asking again, and keeps `said` for a refusal (never shown, never an answer); it takes up to two minutes, so the settings page allows 150 s. A quick job reads a model's notice result until its plan is checked. `APINEX_MODELS` moves to models APInex marks free: quick DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, GLM 5.3 Flash; writing Qwen 3.8 Max, Gemini 3.8 Flash; careful DeepSeek V4 Pro, Kimi K3. | The vault thread read APInex's public model list and rate limits (no key): Claude Sonnet and Opus 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.8, MiniMax M3, MiMo V2.6 Flash and Pro and GLM 5.3 FlashX carry `allowFree: false`, and `free/jev-1.13` is a decision model chat cannot use. The first check also got 402 from models marked free, which the gateway's code gives only when the daily free allowance is spent; keeping APInex's words on the next check says which it is instead of guessing. Before this a 402 on one billed model rested every APInex model for half an hour. Tests: `tests/assist-apinex.test.ts` (refusals, words kept without a key, pacing and Retry-After, not-free carried over a week, the new defaults). |
| D-567 | **APInex is a fourth free provider (`apinex-free`), and the free providers' order now depends on the kind of job, not one fixed list.** Each text task has a free job (`FREE_JOB`): quick (note sort, how-to, column match, cover plan) starts at NVIDIA then APInex; writing (notices, templates, translations, replies, call scripts, summaries) and careful (remarks, a class's results) start at APInex then NVIDIA; OpenRouter and Gemini Free follow. APInex tries at most two models per draft from `APINEX_MODELS` (quick DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, Claude Sonnet 4.6; writing Claude Sonnet 4.6, GLM 5.3; careful Claude Opus 4.6, Sonnet 4.6), with 10/14/18 s each, inside the same 20-24 s free share. Who may reach a free model is unchanged: open tasks, and a test account's text; never a photo. **Settings → AI in One → Cost → Check free models** runs on the server and gives every listed free model the same invented notice and cover plan (`FREE_CHECK_PROBES`) through the real instructions, schema, placeholder and Hindi checks; it keeps only model names, times, a fixed problem word and an HTTP status on `assist_settings.free_check`. For three days after a check, `pickApinexModels` skips a model that failed or was slower than its job allows, and puts the fastest passing models in when none of a job's list passed; a firewall page (HTTP 403 that is not JSON) keeps APInex out for six hours and is never worked around. One model's failure rests that model only; a refused key, no credit, the firewall, the day's allowance or the network rests the whole provider. Any admin may run the check, a practice admin too (it is free and fictional), once in two minutes. The key is `APINEX_API_KEY`, or the vault's `FREE_API_KEY` when it has APInex's shape; per-job overrides `VPPSONE_APINEX_QUICK`/`_WRITING`/`_CAREFUL` take `free/` models only. | The vault's check (10 Nov) listed 42 models, 21 of them free, behind a Cloudflare wall that turns some data-centre callers away; whether Vercel's are let through can only be learnt from Vercel, so the check runs there and says so. The picks are where each model family is strongest: Claude Sonnet for plain Devanagari a parent reads, Opus for reading a class's figures, a Flash model and NVIDIA for short shaped answers; the check then replaces judgement with measurement on One's own tasks. Two models at most keeps a slow gateway from eating the paid models' time. Migration `20261110071216_apinex_free.sql`: `apinex-free` in `assist_log_provider_known`, and `assist_settings.free_check`. Tests: `tests/assist-apinex.test.ts` (routing per job, the check against a fake APInex, the stored shape, firewall and `end_turn`). |

## The school's own WhatsApp in One, AiSensy only in Fees — 9 November 2026

Janmejay, 9 Nov 2026: replace AiSensy in One with the school's own WhatsApp
number, +91 83022 07823, through the school's own server or Meta directly;
never use the Fees app's AiSensy number +91 80 4713 6289; a switch per feature;
nothing to real parents until he approves, and tests only to his own number.
Then: "Don't touch fees app, do it only for one app, let fees app use the
current aisensy setup", and "Yes, use Meta's WhatsApp Cloud API message
format, keep the base URL and key in env vars, and list what's left to fill in."
Then the school's WhatsApp hub's API doc (9 Nov), and: "No, hub only. Don't
touch the Fees app, which stays on AiSensy for 80 4713 6289."

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-566 | **One sends only through the school's own WhatsApp hub (`https://vpps-whatsapp-hub.raj-39e.workers.dev`, repo `veerpatta/vpps-whatsapp-hub`), with Meta's message JSON at `POST /v1/messages` and One's own key, `WHATSAPP_HUB_KEY` (server-only; `WHATSAPP_HUB_URL` defaults to the hub). There is no AiSensy in One and no "school server" transport. The hub's refusals (`sending_off`, `test_mode`, `window_closed`, `opted_out`, `not_approved`, `bad_number`) and Meta's codes (131026, 131047, 131049, 131050, 132000, 132001, 131042) are filed and shown in plain words and on the failure desk; a 502 with Meta's code is a definite no, any other 5xx is unknown. The hub's webhook is `/api/webhooks/whatsapp-hub`, signed with the same key; the queue job also asks `GET /v1/status/{wamid}` up to four times per message. The `_v7` fee reminders go to the hub byte for byte as `_v8`, sent from Settings → WhatsApp → Send for approval; only the hub's list makes a template approved (the manual "Mark approved" is gone), and `APPROVED_HUB` is filled only from APPROVED statuses** | Janmejay, 9 Nov 08:15 UTC: "No, hub only. Don't touch the Fees app, which stays on AiSensy for 80 4713 6289." The hub's API doc (9 Nov) asks for exactly this. `_v7` was approved on AiSensy's account for the Fees number, which One never uses, so the hub's account needs its own copy; building `_v8` from the same descriptors (`domain/hub-templates.ts`) means the two cannot drift, and the sync refuses an approved template whose wording, blanks or button host differ. An approval is never taken from a webhook event alone; a rejection is written at once. The hub starts in test mode (owner's number only) on top of One's own switches (D-563). Migration `20261109082705_whatsapp_hub.sql` (applied to One 9 Nov): template `quality` and `submitted_at`, message `status_polls`/`status_polled_at`, `whatsapp_poll_is_due` and `whatsapp_take_pollable`, the queue timer waking for polls too, and the 22 `_v8` registry rows (`not_submitted`). Tests against a fake hub: `tests/whatsapp-hub.test.ts`. The key was added in Vercel by Janmejay (9 Nov); One's webhook was added to the hub's `APP_WEBHOOKS` by him the same day. |
| D-562 | **One sends WhatsApp only from the school's own number, +91 83022 07823, and only through one service, `src/platform/whatsapp/` (`sendTemplate`, `sendText`, `getStatus`). The body is Meta's WhatsApp Cloud API message JSON, sent either to the school's WhatsApp server (`WHATSAPP_API_URL` + `WHATSAPP_API_KEY`, `POST {url}/messages`) or straight to Meta's Graph API (`WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID` + `WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN`), chosen by the environment alone. One no longer reads `AISENSY_*` or calls AiSensy. The Fees app is untouched and keeps its own AiSensy setup; One refuses the Fees number as a sender and as a recipient** | Every One send path that called AiSensy now goes through the service: fee reminders and their runs, receipts, reversals, fee statements, school notices, One's own parent messages (`/collection`), staff notices and teacher tasks (`claim_teacher_task_delivery` takes transport `whatsapp`, migration `20261109062527`; old `aisensy` rows stay valid history). `data/aisensy.ts` became `data/parent-send.ts`, which still asks the parent-send gate first. The AiSensy webhook route in One is gone; `/api/webhooks/whatsapp` replaces it (Meta's `hub.verify_token` handshake on GET, `X-Hub-Signature-256` HMAC on POST, 404 with no secret set, changes for any other number ignored). `WHATSAPP_SENDER_NUMBER` must be the school's number or every send is refused; `mock` is refused on a live deployment. The school's server is still being built, so its API is assumed to be Meta-compatible; `docs/whatsapp/SETUP.md` lists what to confirm. `tests/whatsapp-platform.test.ts`, `tests/parent-send-gate.test.ts` (only `src/platform/whatsapp/providers.ts` may name a WhatsApp endpoint; no AiSensy endpoint anywhere). |
| D-563 | **Nothing reaches a parent until Janmejay says so in writing. Five switches in `vppsone.runtime_flags`, all closed when they were made: practice mode on (`whatsapp_dry_run`: logged, never sent), every feature off (`whatsapp_features`, eleven features), real recipients off (`whatsapp_real_recipients`: only `whatsapp_owner_test_number` can receive anything), and a daily limit of 250 for every feature together. Real recipients and the owner's number change in the database only, never from a screen; practice mode and the feature switches change in Settings → WhatsApp, for a real admin only** | Owner's direction. The owner's number is stored only in the database (masked on screen), never in the repo (rule 6). The policy is one pure function, `checkWhatsAppSend` (`policy.ts`), asked before every send, under the existing parent-send gate rather than instead of it. A test account may open Settings → WhatsApp and press "Check connection", but every change is refused (`assertNotTestAccount`), as is any change while "See One as" is on. The refusal is a plain note beside the pressed card (`<area>_locked`), never the error screen: on BrowserStack (Galaxy M32, 9 Nov) a practice account's "Turn practice off" first showed "Something went wrong" (`tests/whatsapp-settings-actions.test.ts`). Going live: practice mode off, one feature on, a test to his own number, then `whatsapp_real_recipients` on in the database, in that order, after his go-ahead. |
| D-564 | **A message goes out only under a template whose row in `vppsone.whatsapp_templates` reads `approved`, with the same number of blanks, the same PDF header and the same Pay now button the app sends. Every row starts `not_submitted`: approvals on the Fees app's AiSensy account do not carry over. Template names and blanks come from the registry, not from strings in the code. Settings → WhatsApp shows each feature's approved-template count, and "Check templates" reads approvals from the server or Meta (or an admin marks them)** | Templates are approved per WhatsApp Business Account, so nothing approved for Fees counts here. The seed is what `scripts/whatsapp-template-catalog.mjs` printed from the app's own descriptors; `tests/whatsapp-template-catalog.test.ts` holds the two together, so a descriptor that gains a blank without a registry row fails CI instead of failing silently at send time. No `_v6` template is seeded: its Pay now belongs to the Fees app. A mismatch is a refusal with a plain reason, never a send. |
| D-565 | **`vppsone.whatsapp_messages` is both the log and the queue: one row per message, with every attempt, status and retry appended to `whatsapp_message_events`. A busy server (Meta's 130429, 131000, 131016, 131057) puts a message in `retry_wait`, and the queue (`POST /api/jobs/whatsapp-queue`, every five minutes from `pg_cron` with a Vault secret checked in Postgres) sends it again. A message whose fate is unknown (a 5xx, a timeout, an answer without an id) is never sent again by itself; Settings → WhatsApp offers "Send again" after a warning. Statuses never move backwards. When Razorpay's webhook confirms a payment and One posts it, the message that carried that pay code is marked Paid** | One parent missed beats one parent messaged twice, the rule the AiSensy path already kept (D-352). Each send has an idempotency key, so a repeated request is a `duplicate`, not a second message. Status updates land on the log and on the fee reminder or school notice row that asked for the message (`data/whatsapp-source-sync.ts`). Meta's error codes are shown in plain words with the next step (`errors.ts`). The new tables are classified K in the quarantine rules: made after go-live, never scrubbed. |

## Test accounts only in the test session, real staff only in the real year — 8 November 2026

Janmejay, 8 Nov 2026: "the test accounts and QA accounts should be in the test session only ... the only test sessions will consist of test data, test login IDs, and the real live data will only have the genuine accounts, the real accounts only."

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-561 | **The ported fee engine's 3rd Child step only sends writes One's fee-writes guard can place: "everyone in the family but the one who gets it" is a list of ids (`in(student_id, others)`), never `in(members)` plus `neq(recipient)`, and switching an existing row back on names its child as well as its id. When a link or a take-out from One's own copy stops part-way, One reads the family table before it answers: a family that is already linked (or a child that is already out) is "saved", with "Finish" to complete the 3rd Child Policy and dues, never "nothing was changed". The engine's generated family name ("Family <phone> <id>") is never shown; the guardian's or father's name is** | Live TEST-2026-27 check, 8 Nov 2026: linking three TEST Nursery children made the family, then the guard refused the 3rd Child step (a `neq` on a scope column cannot be placed, an `id`-only update has no scope), the sheet said "This could not be saved. Nothing was changed.", and the new family showed as "Family <phone> 7fc74467". **Fix:** `modules/fees/data/conventional-discounts.ts` (two writes), `linkLocalFamily` / `unlinkLocalFamily` report `settled: false` when the change landed and a later step did not (`linkedTogether`, `unlinkAlreadyLanded` in `domain/family-change.ts`), `familyLabel()` in `vx/screens/families/logic.ts`. Also: the families list had no "Add a brother or sister" button on a phone (the top bar's actions are desk-only); it now has a big one under the search. Tests: `tests/third-child-write-guard.test.ts` runs the real 3rd Child step over a real PostgREST client whose fetch asks the guard about every write (it fails on the old code with two unscoped writes); `tests/vx-family-change.test.ts`. Fees runs the same engine without One's guard and is unaffected. **Later the same day:** the 3rd Child Policy goes to the youngest class even when that child is RTE (lowest tuition wins, so it changes nothing), and the preview then said "Tuition ₹6,000 instead of ₹16,000" and, on taking a child out, "fees go up". Each preview child now carries `thirdChildChangesFees` (false when another active discount already gives tuition no higher than the 3rd Child Policy's), and the sheet says "already pays less tuition with another discount (like RTE), so fees stay the same". One's copy fills it in `previewLocalFamilyChange`; Fees fills it from schoolfees PR 154; a Fees answer without it reads as before. **BrowserStack, 19:30 UTC:** taking out the second-last child said "This family has ended" but the family stayed on the list with one child, because the ported unlink deleted the group by `id` alone (refused, and the error was never read); joining two existing families re-pointed discounts by group id and deleted the absorbed group by id, both refused the same way. `sibling-actions.ts` now reads the rows' own `academic_session_label` and adds it to those three writes (same rows), and a failed clean-up is reported after the dues are worked out, so "Finish" completes it. Test: `tests/sibling-actions-write-guard.test.ts` (fails on the old code). **After that went live:** a save that ends the family (or folds it into an older one by adding a child) re-renders the family page without it before the answer reaches the sheet, so the page showed "This family was not found" and the sheet vanished. `FamilyScreen` now keeps the family as it was when a change it started began saving (`onSaving` on both sheets), the sheet says "Done. This family has ended.", and afterwards the page says "This family has ended" (or "was joined with another"; on a family page the link sheet's Done opens the joined family). Test: `tests/vx-family-ends.test.tsx` (fails on the old code). |
| D-560 | **A session lists only its own kind of staff, whoever is looking: test accounts in a test session (TEST-/UAT-/DEMO-), real staff in a real one, a real admin included. Staff lists, every staff picker and count, "See One as", notes and exam routing, teacher tasks, "who follows this up", the AI page, books and report schedules all ask the same question, and the database refuses the rest** | Before, a real admin in 2026-27 saw all 68 profiles (44 of them practice accounts) on Staff, in every picker and in "See One as"; the notes "coordinator" routed to the QA coordinator because the school has no real one; the staff log, AI usage, UPI lines, deposit slips and report schedules carried QA accounts' rows into the real year. **App:** `staffBelongsToSession()` / `listsTestStaff()` in `platform/auth/test-session-policy.ts`, and `viewingTestSession()` (`platform/auth/staff-scope.ts`), which reads the session pill the same way the session resolvers do; `listStaff()` follows it, so its ~25 callers do. Anyone a real admin adds while looking at TEST-2026-27 is a practice account, and Staff says so in a note at the top ("Test session: practice accounts only"). The `/staff/[id]` page keeps the admin's "mark test account" switch. **Database (migration `20261108170533_staff_session_match.sql`, applied to One 8 Nov):** `vppsone.staff_matches_session()`; `queue_staff_notice` now skips a real person in a test session too (a TEST leave request had queued 14 notices to real staff); `staff_session_match` triggers refuse a test account on real-year class teachers, teaching assignments, timetable slots, staff scopes, cover policies, teacher tasks, collection requests, exam duties and marks links, and a real person on TEST ones, checked only when the person or session changes. A note's "follow up: me" and a plan step's owner are authorship and are not refused. Proved on PGlite by `tests/sql/staff-session-match.mjs` (59 checks, in CI). **Leftovers:** 20 rows from before (16 never-sent notices across the line, two TEST leave rows and one TEST absence recorded for real teachers by QA accounts on the 6 Nov practice day, one real-year cover policy for a test account) are hidden by the same reads (staff log, cover policies, absences, a teacher's own leave, the timetable's leave list); deleting them waits on the owner's OK in the Supabase connector's confirmation, which got no answer. Nothing in the real year about a real person was changed. D-556 to D-559 left for the Families thread running the same day. |

## Families: brothers and sisters with their fees together — 8 November 2026

Janmejay, 8 Nov 2026: "configure the families thing in the one app and fix it as it is not reachable right now in the one app. So, fix the family section and fix it end to end perfectly and deploy it to live." Then: "Yes, one should also be able to link and unlink the siblings."

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-556 | **Families is One's own screen, `/families` (Menu → Children & staff → Families, the desk's side menu under People, and the accountant's menu), opened by `fees:view`: every family of the year with its children's faces, the parent, each child's class and the money in words ("₹12,000 fees left", "Fees paid", the late fee on its own line), one search box for a child, a parent, a phone or an SR (typed, in Hindi, or spoken), filters with counts and "Most fees left". A family opens `/families/[groupId]`: Call and WhatsApp, Collect fee, the year's fees for all the children together, and each child with fees left, paid, late fee, discount, next due, Open child and Collect fee. Student 360's Family tab gets "Open family page"** | The old tile went to `/student-tools?view=families`, which the Fees redirect sends real staff to "Not in One yet": the section could not be opened on the real year. The new screen reads Fees itself for real staff on a real year (`GET /api/one/v1/screens/families`, schoolfees PR 152: groups, members, records and the workbook money, fees and late fee apart) and One's copy for test accounts and TEST years, in one shape (`buildFamilyBook`), and never draws One's copy when Fees did not answer. A child with no money row makes the family total "not read", never a smaller number; a family whose only debt is a late fee has its fees paid |
| D-557 | **On the real year the Families screens only read; linking and separating brothers and sisters from One comes next, through a Fees command with a fee-change preview before saving. TEST years keep "Change brothers and sisters" (`/student-tools?view=families`, `fees:manage`)** | Linking moves the third-child discount and regenerates dues, so it must run in Fees through its own sibling actions, from a narrowly scoped `/api/one/v1` command with the actor's grant, never from One's clients. The owner said yes to linking from One (8 Nov); it is built as its own step so the read screen could ship first |
| D-558 | **Brothers and sisters are linked and separated on One's Families screens: "Add a brother or sister" on the families list and on a family's page, "Take out of this family" on each child. Admins only (`fees:manage` and the Fees student page's `students:write`), never while seeing One as someone else. Two steps: pick the children (search by name, SR or father, typed or spoken), then "Check fees" shows what the change will do before anything is saved: who is in the family after, who gets the 3rd Child Policy and their new tuition, who loses it, each child's fees left and late fee apart. "Yes, link them" / "Yes, take out" saves. Supersedes D-557's TEST-only "Change brothers and sisters" link** | Owner, 8 Nov 2026: "one should also be able to link and unlink the siblings." Linking moves the 3rd Child Policy and redraws dues, so a person sees that first, in words, with big buttons, a spinner while Fees works it out and a tick when saved (`cue()` on each step). The real year goes to Fees (D-559); test accounts and TEST years change One's own copy with the ported Fees sibling engine (`modules/students-fees/vx/family-change.ts`), and the preview there is the same prediction (`domain/family-change.ts`, ported from Fees `predictFamilyChange`) with one difference: One's ported engine has no "Staff Child outranks the 3rd Child Policy" rule yet, so its preview does not predict it (`staffChildOutranks: false`). Tests: `tests/vx-family-change.test.ts`. |
| D-559 | **On the real year a link or an unlink is a Fees command, `POST /api/one/v1/families/preview`, `/link` and `/unlink` (schoolfees PR 153), run as the person with their own Fees grant. The save carries the household the preview showed (`expectedMemberIds`): if anyone changed one of those families in between, Fees refuses (`family_changed`) and One says "Check again", never overwrites. Each save has a command id; a lost answer is retried with the same id and never links twice. A preview is never treated as a lost save** | Fees stays the financial authority (coexistence, 1 Nov): the change runs in Fees' own household engine and its 3rd Child and dues steps, never from One's clients. One's transport lets through only these three family routes, and only link and unlink count as commands. The Fees side needs one migration on the live Fees database (source functions and a `can_link_family` grant flag), applied only on the owner's go-ahead. Until then Fees answers `family_commands_disabled` and One says linking "is not switched on yet" and that nothing was changed, never "try again". **Applied 9 Nov 2026 03:33 UTC** on the owner's go-ahead ("Yes apply migrations in fees app as well"), as `20261109033321_one_bridge_family_commands` (schoolfees PR 155; the Supabase MCP stamps its own version). `can_link_family` is on for the two real Fees admins' grants only; the QA admin's grant on 2026-27 stays off, because test accounts work only in the test year (D-560). A no-write preview on the live Fees database allowed both admins and refused the QA admin, an accountant and a TEST-year request (`bridge:forbidden`). Off switch: `update private.one_bridge_grants set can_link_family = false;`. No One code changed: `familiesCanChange` already showed the buttons to a real admin. |

## See One as a chosen person — 7 November 2026

Janmejay, 7 Nov 2026: "as an admin, when I wanted to see the app from the view of a teacher or academic coordinator, I should also have an access to see and select which teacher or which academic coordinator so I can actually see what the teacher is saying and actually see what the academic coordinator or accountant is seeing and their real data can be shown to me, only the admin … Deploy all the changes to live and apply migrations if needed." Then, going to sleep: "do the recommended approach and you have all my approvals".

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-544 | **An administrator chooses a member of staff — Menu → "See One as…" → Choose a person, the sidebar, or "See One as {name}" on their Staff profile — and One draws every screen as that person sees it, with their real data: their menu, start screen, classes, tasks, inbox and language. Nothing can be saved while looking. An amber banner names the person on every screen, with "Change person" and "Back to my view", and the view ends by itself after two hours.** | The role preview (UI-12, "See One as the office") shows a role's menu over the administrator's own data, which is not what a teacher sees. **How:** a cookie `vx_view_person` = `<administrator's auth id>.<staff id>`, bound to the administrator who set it, so a shared office PC never shows the next person anybody else's screens. `getCurrentStaff()` returns the chosen person with `viewedBy` = the administrator; `getSignedInStaff()` is the real person, for the switch, its banner and log, and the Fees bridge's token. One's data clients send `x-vx-view-as`, and the database answers as that person (`vppsone.view_as_subject_id()`), but only inside a READ ONLY transaction backed by an open, unexpired `vppsone.view_as_log` row that the administrator started (`view_as_begin()`). Migration `20261107164847`, applied to One's database; its comments say D-520 because PR 301 took that number before this merged. **Saves are refused five times over:** `assertNotPreviewing()` (in the administrator's language); the server data clients (One's and the fee module's, user and service role); the proxy, for non-GET `/api/*` and `/documents/*`; the AI helper; and the browser data clients, which refuse uploads while the page carries `data-vx-view-as`. The Fees bridge always speaks with the administrator's own token. **Who may be seen:** any active member of staff who is not an administrator; a test administrator only test accounts; a Smoke Lab account nobody. Every look is logged (who, whom, when, how long) and the picker lists the last four. The view's language can be switched without saving it, and each person opens in their own. Tests: `tests/view-as-person.test.ts`. |

## Transport: the children on each route and each bus, and who drives — 7 November 2026

Janmejay, 7 Nov 2026: "I can only see on bus, and the filters as well, I can only see on bus, but there are so many transport routes … so I can effectively see the students on different times of routes, and assign drivers on the routes as well, so I can know which driver is going on which route, so implement this and deploy all of the changes to live." Then: "do the recommended approach and you have all my approvals", and "I am ok with all the database changes" (16:52 UTC).

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-550 | **Transport is one screen, `/transport` (Menu → "Buses & routes", the desk's side menu → Transport), with three views of the same facts: Routes (a card per route with its children and the buses that come to it, their time and driver, and "No morning bus yet" when children wait for one), Bus times (every run by time of day, morning, afternoon or another, with its driver, its routes in order and "Carries N children") and Drivers (each driver, the buses they drive, a Call button). Tapping a route or a bus opens its children by class. One search box finds a route, a bus, a driver or a child, and a child found brings their route** | Before this, the only transport view in One was Students' "On bus" filter (the Menu's "Bus students" tile opened it): one list of every child who rides, with no route, time or driver. Which route a child is on was already in the nightly roster copy (`transport_route_id`); what was missing was a screen that groups by it, and any record of buses and drivers. A child rides every bus that stops at their route, so "who is on the 7:05 bus" and "which driver goes to this village" are worked out (`buildBoard`), never stored twice. The filters are the owner's questions: routes with no morning or no afternoon bus, buses by time of day, buses with no driver. Fees' "No Transport" row is a placeholder and never counted as a route. The Students "On bus" filter stays. |
| D-551 | **Fees keeps the routes and which child is on which; One keeps the drivers and the bus runs. The route names are copied from Fees (`GET /api/one/v1/sync/routes`, schoolfees PR 151: names, codes and flags, never a fee) into `fees_bridge_routes`, replaced whole after the nightly roster job, after an admin's "Refresh roster now", from the screen's "Get route names again" (at most once in ten minutes), and once when the copy is empty or over 26 hours old. Drivers (`transport_drivers`) and runs (`transport_trips`, `transport_trip_stops`) belong to one session, so TEST-2026-27 has its own** | Moving a child to another route stays a change in Fees, and the screen says so: two places that could each move a child would soon disagree. The names ride on the roster's nightly rhythm (D-457) and never get a timer of their own. A TEST session reads its own local route list and never asks Fees. The new session tables carry the restrictive `test_session_fence` like One's other session tables; the app's fence is `assertSessionWritable` (D-179). Until the tables exist on a database, the screen still shows the children per route and says the buses are being set up (`missingTable`). |
| D-552 | **Adding a bus is one form in plain steps: time of day, leaves at, bus name and number, driver ("+ A new driver" adds one in the same Save), helper, then the routes in the order the bus reaches them, each with an optional time, and "This bus carries N children" as they are added. "Make the afternoon run from this" copies a morning bus with its routes the other way round. Nothing is sent until every box is right, and an answer that was lost is never sent again** | The whole bus is checked on the phone and again on the server before anything is written, so a refused bus leaves no driver behind; a driver made just before a failed save is handed back to the form, so a second Save cannot add them twice. `transport_save_trip` writes a bus and its stops in one transaction with an `updated_at` check, so two people changing one bus cannot overwrite each other. When the answer is lost (a phone going offline mid-save), the form says it may have saved and to look at the list, never "Try again": a second copy of a bus is the one mistake a person cannot see from the form. Removing asks on the same sheet, never in a second one. Big labelled buttons, a spinner and a tick on Save, `cue()` on save and on error, a "speak your note" box for notes, and Call buttons that ring the number. |
| D-553 | **Two switches on Staff → Roles: `transport:view` (open Transport) and `transport:manage` (add and change buses and drivers), on for the admin and the office. The Menu's "Bus students" tile becomes "Buses & routes"** | Migration `20261107165001`. The office had the old tile, so it gets the new screen; any other role is one switch away. Accountants and fee collectors, who saw the old tile through `fees:view`, keep Students' "On bus" filter. Every server action checks `transport:manage`, refuses a "see the app as" preview, and refuses when the session the screen showed is not the account's session now or one it may write. Children's photos and the links to their pages show only to a reader with `students:view`. The two migrations' headers say D-521, the number they were written under before PR 301 took it; applied migrations are not edited. |
| D-554 | **A child's photo is shown wherever One shows that child as a circle (class lists, attendance, marks, notes, the payment desk, collect, search, birthdays, siblings, academics, transport), and a tap on a photo opens it large, growing out of the circle the way a profile photo opens in WhatsApp; Close, a tap outside, a swipe down, Escape or Back shrinks it back. Photos come at one address per child per day, at list size (192 px), and the phone keeps them** | Owner, 8 Nov 2026: teachers saw initials where photos should be, and a photo "takes time to load". One handed out five-minute signed links, new on every page, so a phone could never reuse a face it had already fetched and every list downloaded every photo again at 600 px. **How:** `/api/photos/students/<child>?v=<photo row>&d=<day>&s=list|full&t=<token>` (`modules/collection/domain/photo-link.ts`): `t` is an HMAC of child, photo row and day, keyed from the service-role key with a label, and made only after the reader's permission was checked (`approvedStudentPhotos`, unchanged rules). The route checks the token, not the reader again, and answers `private, immutable` until the link expires (end of the next UTC day), so fifty faces cost fifty plain image requests once a day. A replaced photo has a new row id, so a new address at once. Screens that list children without loading photos give the child's id to `Face` (`vx/ui/face.tsx`), and `vx/ui/student-photos.ts` asks `/api/photos/students?ids=` for every face drawn in one go (a GET, so it works while an admin sees One as someone, D-544) and remembers the answers for the visit. The open photo (`vx/ui/face-zoom.tsx`) is loaded on the first press of a face and starts the sharper photo on the press; the list photo is drawn at once and the sharp one fades in over it. Light `cue("tap")`, reduced motion fades instead of growing. `Face` and its photo lookup ship as one small shared chunk (`vx-faces`, a cache group in `next.config.ts`), because webpack would otherwise copy them into every page that draws a face; a design `Avatar` that shows a child is `ChildAvatar` (`vx/ui/child-avatar.tsx`), so the shell's own avatar carries none of it. Canonical (live) readers get One's copy of the Fees photo when it is up to date and Fees' own photo route otherwise, so a changed Fees photo is never shown stale. The child's page keeps "Save photo". Tests: `tests/student-photo-links.test.ts`, `tests/fees-bridge-shared-photos.test.ts`, `tests/profile-photo-readers.test.ts`. |
| D-555 | **The teachers' photo copy (D-511) carries on by itself: a few seconds after a real admin opens One, the same copy "Refresh roster now" runs starts quietly in the background, and keeps going batch after batch until every Fees photo is in One. One copy runs at a time; a tap on the button while it runs joins it and shows its bar** | Owner, 8 Nov 2026. The first copy stopped after one batch at 279 of 484 children, when the admin left the screen, and nothing ever carried on, so about half of every class showed initials to its teacher. A batch that fails is asked for once more from the same place, and the button now says what is left ("The rest are copied the next time an admin opens One"). **Not a schedule (D-457):** nothing runs unless an admin has One open (the copy needs that admin's own grant at Fees); the server asks One's own database what is missing first, so Fees is read only while a photo is still to copy; and each device remembers when to look again (`vx_photo_copy_next`): six hours once everything is copied, three minutes after a run cut short, an hour when Fees could not be reached, a day when Fees cannot hand some photos over, so a broken photo never makes every open page through Fees. Never for a test account, a role preview or "See One as" (`platform/fees-bridge/photo-catch-up-run.ts`, started by the roster card's button). The button and the copy are fetched after the page (`lazy-refresh-roster-button.tsx`), so no page carries them up front. Tests: `tests/teacher-photo-copy.test.ts`. |

## Certificates on the school's letterhead, signed and sealed, and a teacher's request approved by the admin — 7 November 2026

Janmejay, 7 Nov 2026: "redesign the certificate letterhead that can be made in the one app, in all one app … add the signature of the principal from the receipts and make a school seal as well … when the certificate is generated from an approved staff member or admin, the sign of the principal and the school seal must be there, and if the certificate is prepared from the teacher, then an approval is needed … the approval is sent to the admin for printing, sign and school seal … after the approval, the teacher can directly download the certificate and send to the parent if needed."

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-540 | **Every certificate One makes (study, character, date of birth, TC, and a tray's bundle) is set on the school's official letterhead: the crest at the head, flanked by the registration and recognition numbers on the left and the UDISE and PSP codes on the right, the school's name in English and Hindi, the trust, and one line of address, phone, email and website, over the navy-and-gold double rule** | The letterhead is the school's own `VPPS_Letterhead_Blank_A4.pdf` (Google Drive, August 2026), and the school's paper certificates on Drive use it; One's printed the name and logo only. Its lines are the defaults of Certificate settings (`OFFICIAL_LETTERHEAD` in `modules/certificates/domain/identity.ts`), so a school that never opened the settings prints its real letterhead; a manager can change any line and a line saved empty is left off. A new issue freezes them into its snapshot, so an older document reprints as it was issued. The letterhead's address is the receipts' ("Amet-Kelwa Road, Amet … 313332", D-505) with the letterhead's district: **amended 7 Nov 2026, the owner's answer**, replacing the Drive letterhead's "Opposite Mela Ground … 313330", so a certificate and a receipt give one address. The email is the receipts' `info@vpps.co.in`. A long name is set smaller rather than wrapped (`nameSize`). Synthetic fixtures only in tests. |
| D-541 | **A certificate prepared, or approved, by someone allowed to issue certificates carries the principal's printed signature (the one on every receipt) and the school seal; the seal is a new round rubber-stamp design: "Shri Veer Patta Sr. Sec. School" around the rim, "Amet (Rajsamand)" below between two stars, the crest in the middle, in stamp-pad violet and a little uneven at the edges, as ink is** | Owner's request. `signaturePolicy` now defaults to `facsimile` (it was `manual`, a blank line and an empty circle to sign and stamp by hand); turning it back on after a switch to `manual` records who did it and when, as before. The snapshot names the signature and the seal rather than embedding them (`certificateSigned`), so the register, the detail page and the student's card say "Signed" and the signing tray becomes "To hand over" for such documents; a document issued before this keeps its blank line and empty circle and is still signed by hand. The seal is drawn beside the signature, overlapping its edge, where a clerk stamps it (`public/branding/school-seal.png`, `loadSealImage`). The principal's name is not printed, only the designation, until the school enters it in Certificate settings (entered 7 Nov 2026 from the owner's answer). |
| D-542 | **A teacher asks; an admin approves and signs; the teacher downloads the signed copy and sends it to the family** | Two new permissions on Staff → Roles, in a "Certificates" group that also gives the three older certificate permissions real names (they sat under "Other" as raw keys): `certificates:request` ("Ask for a certificate": teachers and the admin by default) and `certificates:approve` ("Approve certificate requests": the admin by default, grantable to another role, and useful only with `certificates:issue`). **Asking:** on a child's page, a big "Ask for a certificate" button opens three choices (study, character, date of birth; a TC stays with the office's leaving workflow), the conduct for a character certificate, an optional purpose in the document's language and a note the teacher can type or speak; sending prepares nothing. Only a child in one of the teacher's own classes, a TEST year from a test account, one open request per child and document, and a lost answer replays the same request (`command_id`). **Approving:** the queue "Asked by teachers" is at the top of School office, with a card on the admin's Today; each request shows the father's name and date of birth to check (the teacher's screens never get them), "Approve and sign" issues the document through the ordinary issue path under the approver's own name (signed and sealed by D-541) and links it, and "Decline" needs a reason the teacher reads. The issue's command id is derived from the request and the approver, so a retry after a lost answer replays the same document instead of numbering a second; a record the issue path refuses lists what to fix, with a link to the child's record. **Downloading:** the teacher's card on the child's page shows Waiting, Ready, Declined or "copy cancelled"; Ready offers "Send to parent" (the phone's own share list, WhatsApp among it, started by the teacher and never sent by the app) and Download, and each one is written into the certificate's history first. Today lists her own requests in the same words, each opening that child's page; it is drawn on the server and streams in after the rest of Today, so the screen every teacher opens first carries no extra JavaScript. The PDF is the stored original, checked against its recorded hash, never re-rendered. All of it is in `vppsone.school_certificate_request_*` (service role only; migrations `…_certificate_requests.sql` and `…_certificate_requests_role_permissions.sql`), proved on PGlite by `tests/sql/certificate-requests.mjs` in CI. Two approvers pressing at the same moment can leave one extra issued copy in the register (the second link is refused); rare enough to cancel by hand. |
| D-543 | **A certificate is always one A4 sheet** | The page is drawn at the roomiest of three settings that fits, worked out from the length of what it carries, so a long two-language TC steps down to a tighter setting rather than spilling the signature onto a second page. `tests/school-office-pdf.test.ts` renders the longest TC in each language, a two-language character certificate with a purpose and a three-page bundle, and counts pages. |

## One on a desk: every screen uses the width it is given — 7 November 2026

Janmejay, 7 Nov 2026: "Also have a look at the desktop view of the HoloOne app and optimize any changes, all the changes we have done so far, for desktop as well and optimize the UI and UX for the desktop beautifully, the widgets that are shown, everything, all the features completely optimized well and fully for the desktop, and test it as well. Brainstorm creative ideas and deploy all the changes tonight."

Every screen was checked on a desk at 1920, 1366, 1100 and 1000 pixels wide (with and without the side menu's narrow mode) and on a 390-pixel phone. The phone layouts are unchanged; each fix below is scoped to the desk by a container query or by `only-d`.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-528 | **On a desk, Fees today follows the teaching cards in the wide column while the rail runs beside both, and the money cards arrange themselves by the section's own width** | Fees today began only where the rail ended, so at 1366 up to ~600px of the wide column stood empty above it, and How they pay stretched beside the class table to ~650px of card around one line. The grid's rows are now `auto auto 1fr` with the rail spanning the last two: a spanning item gives its extra height only to flexible tracks, so the money starts 20px under the teaching cards whatever the rail's length. The section is its own container (`tdm` in `today.css`): with 900px or more the chart sits beside The year's pace and How they pay, the class table under both; narrower, the two small cards pair up under the chart; under 560px they stack. How they pay with nothing taken yet draws a dashed ring at full size and says cash, UPI and cheques appear as the day's receipts come in, so the card neither looks broken nor jumps at the first receipt. The year's pace ends with two figures, "Still to come this year" and "Due by today". The section's own words live in `today-money.tsx`, the chunk only a desk loads, not in `TD`, and the desk's "+ New" menu moved to a chunk of its own (`today-new.tsx`) the same way: the home page's chunk counts against every route's first-load budget, and `/teach/attendance` had about 30 bytes to spare (D-510). The greeting's session line is said on the server (`page.tsx`) and no longer ships to the browser. `tests/mobile-ui/today-desktop-widgets.spec.ts` pins the position at 1366, the pairing at 1180 and the phone's order |
| D-529 | **The Students table fits a 1366 and a 1100 desk with its Call / WhatsApp / Collect buttons in view, and its column names stay under the top bar while a long class scrolls** | The fixed column minimums summed to 1,078px, which cut the row's buttons off on a 1366 laptop beside the side menu and half of them at 1100. Every column can now give width back (name from 180px, figures from 72–88px), so the table fits from 780px of columns. Wherever the desk table shows (from 900px of page, 820px inside its card) the card clips instead of scrolling sideways, which lets the header row stick at 64px under the top bar. It first went live clipping only from 1,100px; the BrowserStack check that evening, a 1250px Windows window beside the full side menu, showed the column names scrolling away, and so would a 1080p laptop at 150% (1280px). Measured live from 1000 to 1450 wide with large text off and on, the table never needed to scroll sideways from 900px, because container queries count large text's zoom, so the threshold is now 900px |
| D-530 | **The desk side menu says when more is below, keeps the open screen's item in view, gives a long name a second line, and fetches a screen only on intent** | On a 1366x768 laptop an admin's menu runs past the fold with no scrollbar, so Settings, Staff and Timetable were out of sight with nothing to say so. The list now fades at whichever edge runs on, and a small "More below · नीचे और भी" chip scrolls it on (its arrow bobs twice; no motion under reduced motion). Both are drawn by CSS from the menu's own scroll (a named `scroll-timeline` with `animation-range`), with no script: a menu that fits shows neither, and a browser without scroll-driven animations shows the plain list as before. The open page's item is scrolled into view when it is out of sight. A name like "Cover for absent teachers" wraps instead of losing its end. The items move from Next's default prefetch to `@/ui/link` (D-439's intent rule): D-439 kept the default for the shell because the five phone tabs are where people go next, but the desk menu is the whole app, and each page load rendered every item in view ahead of the click. The phone tabs keep the default. `tests/intent-prefetch-link.test.tsx` fails if the sidebar goes back to `NavLink` |
| D-531 | **School settings lays the form out by the panel's width: beside the live receipt the field pairs stack under 960px, and the receipt moves under the form below 640px** | On a 1366 laptop the panel is about 760px, and two fields side by side beside the 360px receipt cut the school's name to "Shri Veer Patta Sr. S" and the Upload buttons in half. Under 960px the receipt also narrows from 360px to 300px. A container on the panel (`stc`) replaces the window breakpoint, so the same rules hold in the side menu's narrow mode and under large text |
| D-532 | **Admissions on a desk: SR numbers take one line, the document columns hold their pills, and a pipeline card's Admit or Move button wraps under its line rather than out of the card** | In a 64px column a TEST SR number ran down four lines; the SR column now takes 96px or more and cuts with "…" (the whole number is in its tooltip), the date has a fixed 84px, and the three 92px document columns are 118px, so "Birth · pending" fits its pill. In the pipeline's narrow columns at 1000px, "9 days · call back" and the button now share two lines, the button on the right |
| D-533 | **A teacher's Notes on a desk has a big "New note" button in its top card, and the floating one comes in only once that has scrolled away** | On a desk the floating button sat over the corners of the notes and was the only way to start one, far from where the eye starts. The top card carries a 52px New note button on a desk; an IntersectionObserver hides the floating button while the top one is in view and brings it back after. A phone never shows the top one, so its floating button stays as it was |

## Needs you · Teachers' work: collecting from teachers the Sampark way — 7 November 2026

Janmejay, 7 Nov 2026: "have a look at the needs you section for admins … where assigning the work to the teachers … brainstorm creative ideas on how to make this better and quickly for the admin to collect the data from the teachers, the sampark way, and you know, the links to be sent to the teachers and collecting the data quickly, and collecting the missing data quickly … rework the UI and the UX and deploy the changes to live". Then: "do the recommended approach and you have all my approvals".

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-520 | **Needs you has two halves, "To decide" and "Teachers' work", as two big tabs that are links (`/inbox`, `/inbox?view=collect`). Teachers' work is one board: a "Get details from teachers" card (Ask → Send link → Check, and the Ask teachers button), three next steps with their counts (Links to send, Teachers to remind, Answers to check), a late banner, then each request with every teacher as one row and one word for where they stand. "To decide" keeps a small Teachers' work card with the same three counts** | Before, the work handed to teachers was a list of rows under the decisions, and the office had to open each teacher's sheet to find the link and send it, one at a time. Tasks made by one "Give work" share kind, title and `created_at` (one transaction), so the board groups them back into the request the office remembers making. Only `collection:manage` sees the second half, as before. The counts are pure rules (`domain/collect.ts`, 23 tests): a link is owed when the task waits, was never sent and never opened; a reminder is owed when the teacher has the link, has not finished, and was not reminded today. |
| D-521 | **Send links and Remind open a send list in the Sampark way: every teacher on one screen with one green button that opens WhatsApp with their own message and link already typed, a "Next" marker, "1 of 4 done", Copy message and See the message. The tap is noted through the existing `prepare_teacher_task_delivery` → `queue_teacher_task_delivery` → `claim_teacher_task_delivery` (manual), so the board knows who was told. Nothing is sent by the app** | The button is a real `wa.me` link built when the list opens, so the phone opens WhatsApp at once (no second "Open WhatsApp" step, nothing for a pop-up blocker to stop). A link sent today is not offered as a reminder until the next school day (Asia/Kolkata) unless the work is due today or late, so a teacher is never nudged twice in a morning. A send whose result is `unknown` blocks the list for that teacher and points to the task's own sheet, the same rule the sheet enforces. A teacher with no usable mobile is said plainly, with Copy message. The message is in the teacher's language (`en`, `hi`, or both line by line), says what is asked, how many children, the due day, how far they got (reminders), and that no login is needed. Messages to parents are untouched. |
| D-522 | **Ask teachers is four numbered questions instead of a form: What do you need? (every detail says how many children are missing it), For which children? (only those missing it, or every child, by class chips in school order), Who fills it in? (each child's class teacher, recommended, or one teacher for all; house, route and subject under "Other ways"), By when? (Tomorrow, In 3 days, In a week, a date, or none). "12 children · 3 teachers" is worked out as you tap with the server's own `planTasks`. After "Give work to N teachers", the send list opens in the same sheet** | The old sheet was selects and a whole-school checkbox list, with no sign of what was missing. Now asking for missing data is the default: "Only children missing it" sends teachers exactly those children. In the "who gets it" step, changing a teacher re-checks the list in place, and only teachers the server accepts for that group are offered (the old select offered everyone and then blocked the group). A group with two class teachers is picked there; a group with none says to use "One teacher for all" or set the class teacher in Staff. Marks and attendance keep the same questions, fewer of them. |
| D-523 | **No migration, no new writer, no change to what teachers see. The board reads the existing task list (now with `created_at`), `message_sends` and the delivery summary and share-link functions; words are pairs in `collect-words.ts`; the send list loads only when opened** | The send list and the Ask teachers sheet are lazy (`import()`), so Needs you's first load carries neither. The bundle budgets pass. The teacher's own screens and links are unchanged; the In-app vs Sampark-link marks rule (both kept) is unchanged. |

## One name for each subject, and a filled paper that says it is not sent — 7 November 2026

Janmejay, 7 Nov 2026, going to sleep: "do all the possible work as much as possible … do the recommended approach". A second pass over the teacher screens on a 360 px phone, live on TEST-2026-27 as the QA teacher (reads Hindi) and QA teacher 2 (reads English), after D-511 to D-518.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-524 | **A subject has one name in each language on every screen a teacher opens: "Maths" / "गणित", "ELGA" / "अंग्रेज़ी भाषा", "Social Sci." / "सामाजिक विज्ञान"** | The second pass found one lesson named four ways: "कक्षा 1 · ELGA" on Today, "Class 1 ELGA" on My subjects, "कक्षा 1 · अंग्रेज़ी भाषा" on its marks card, and the code "MATHS" on the profile chips and the class's latest marks; Today said "Mathematics" where Marks said "Maths". Today, My class, My subjects and More read the subject list, whose Hindi column is an English copy for 22 of 23 subjects, while the marks and exam screens read the word list in `exams/domain/groups.ts`. `subjectWords(code, typed)` is now the one rule. English is `subjectName(code)`, what the marks, exam and report screens already said; a name typed on the subject's record is used only when that rule has nothing better than the code itself (a subject added later, say "PHE" typed as "Physical Education"). Hindi is the word list's, then a name typed in Devanagari, then the English. `subjectNamer(list)` matches codes without case, so the timetable's "Maths" and the list's "MATHS" are one subject. `subjectName("ELGA")` is "ELGA": the capitals rule had read the acronym as the word "Elga", which the report card printed too. The office marks sheet's title and notice say the subject's name rather than its code. Test: `tests/teacher-one-subject-name.test.ts`. |
| D-525 | **A paper with every mark in but not sent says so wherever a teacher looks: "Filled · not sent" on Today's link card and More's links, "All entered · not sent" on My subjects, "1 paper filled, not sent" on More's Marks tile, and "Open, check and send" on the link sheet's button** | More's Marks tile read "Nothing waiting", Today's link card "सभी 5 पूरे ✓" and My subjects "जारी" (in progress) over a paper whose five marks were all in and never sent to the exam office, so the one step left was hidden from the person who had to take it. `linkStanding` (sent, sent with blanks, filled, started, nothing yet) decides all four places, and More's links view names the next step ("tap Check & send"). |
| D-526 | **Words fit a 360 px phone: the centre button's word may use its neighbours' empty space, the teacher top bar's line and a child's name in the register get two lines before "…", and My subjects says "4 subjects · 5 classes" under its title** | The centre word read "Attendan…": it needs about 76 px and a column on a 360 px phone has 70, while the neighbours' words ("Class", "Notes") leave about 17 px free on each side, so the word may now grow 12 px into each. The top bar's line under the title cut "Attendance · Sat 7 Nov …" and "ELGA, EVS, Mathematics, …", and the register cut long names, which is how a teacher tells two children apart; both now wrap to a second line (`.tc-el2`). The list of subjects stays the first line of the page, in full. |
| D-527 | **On "My tasks" a marks paper says which exam it is: "अंक · कक्षा 1 · पर्यावरण" with "अर्धवार्षिक परीक्षा · अंतिम तिथि 14 अक्टूबर" under it, the oldest exam first, then classes in school order** | With First formative and Half yearly both open, a teacher's list read "अंक · Class 1 · EVS" twice, with nothing to tell the two apart, English codes on a Hindi screen, and the papers in database-id order. The paper query now reads its exam's names and start day, and the exam office's "marks due" day by exam id (`exam_plans` points at `assessments` twice, through its exam and `copied_from`, so an embed is ambiguous and would fail every load). Class and subject use the teacher screens' one rule (D-524). The extra fields ride a `TeacherTaskSummary` beside `TaskSummary`, so the teacher-data change (PR 301), which rewrote `TaskSummary` at the same time, merged beside it without a clash. Test: `tests/teacher-tasks-exam-name.test.ts`. |
| D-538 | **A subject card finds its paper by the class's id, and a paper's class is named from the class list ("Class 11 Arts"), on My subjects, Today, More and My tasks** | The live check of D-524 to D-526 as QA teacher 2 found every Class 11 and 12 card on My subjects reading "No paper yet", with its Marks button opening the empty marks page, while the list below it showed the same papers ("11 Arts · Hindi, 0 of 5 sent"). Papers keep the label they were made with ("11 Arts"); the class list, with the school's own names, says "Class 11 Arts"; the card matched the two as text. Marks links now carry `classId` and the class list's name, the card matches on `classId`, and My tasks names classes the same way. Test: `tests/teacher-tasks-exam-name.test.ts`. First numbered D-528 and then D-534, which PRs 305 and 306 took. |
| D-539 | **Today's "Mark attendance" button calls with a ring that grows off it and fades, four times, then rests; the button itself never fades** | It faded to 55% every 2.4 s to draw the eye. On a 360 px phone that reads as a button switched off, and the fade overrode the dimming a tapped link shows while its page loads (D-514). A `tc-call` class carries the ring on a `::after` (transform and opacity only, so a low-end phone draws it without repainting), with its own reduced-motion rule because vx.css's reaches elements, not pseudo-elements.
