| D-384 | **The AI connection (MCP) lives inside One, as route handlers over a portable core** (`src/modules/mcp/domain`: Web `Request` → `Response`, Web Crypto, an injected store). There is no Cloudflare gateway for now | D5, "whichever works best". Every tool must run One's own permission and scope code against One's database, so a Worker could only proxy. That would mean an extra hop, a new gateway↔One shared secret and a second deploy, and the dependency on Vercel would remain. The owner's Vercel Pro is for this month only: when One's hosting moves, the MCP moves with it. Workers Free stays the evaluated fallback (feasibility §8.2). The handler's CPU time has not been measured against its 10 ms limit |
| D-385 | **One is its own OAuth 2.1 authorization server. Its tokens and codes are opaque 256-bit values, stored only as SHA-256 in One's Postgres** (`vppsone.ai_*`: RLS on, no policies, service role only). Access tokens last 15 minutes. Refresh tokens rotate inside a 90-day grant, and reuse revokes the connection | Single-statement updates and row/advisory locks make codes single-use, rotate refresh tokens and revoke at once. That needs no KV, D1 or Durable Objects (KV is eventually consistent). There are no signing keys, secrets or env vars. Supabase's OAuth server was not used: its tokens are the ordinary session (`aud: authenticated`, no custom scopes). Migration `20261104022925` was applied and checked |

The service-role twins of RLS reads require `readsEveryClass`, so a teacher stage needs class-scoped twins first |

After: 1.36–2.2 s for first-time terms and 0.58–0.68 s for repeats. The 500 ms target is still not met |

This stays only if live measurement shows it is not slower.** If it is slower, revert One #184; Fees keeps the workspace route for one release | P02: #184 and Fees #137. Query counts fall for every reader except a full-permission open (~59 against 49). The first live sample, a 4.3 s page on a cold server, does not establish the effect |

## School Office second pass — 3 November 2026

The owner found the first School Office build unsatisfying and asked for every
brainstorm recommendation (docs/plans/2026-11-03-school-office-brainstorm.md)
to be built, the certificates to look good, and a live release. Decisions made
here (rule 8), each reversible in Certificate settings or a later template:

- **Four kinds, each its own template, series and reprint-first rule:** study
  (VPPS/STUDY, existing approved wording kept), character (VPPS/CC), date of
  birth (VPPS/DOB) and TC (VPPS/TC). New default wording is active so the office
  can use it; a manager edits it as a new version. A purpose line prints only
  when the office gives one. Gendered words come from the record and stay
  neutral ("child", "the student") when gender is blank; nothing is guessed.
- **A TC is a document, not the leaving act.** It is refused (`not_left`) until
  the student's enrolment already has a leaving date; reason and conduct are
  required at the counter. Fee dues are not checked or printed: whether dues
  may block a TC is a school policy nobody has stated. A fee (80C) certificate
  is not built: receipts are not in One until the cutover, and it belongs to the
  fee module.
- **The paper carries no workflow notice.** "Signature required" moved from the
  PDF into the register status; the page has a signature line, the principal's
  printed name and designation, a seal circle and a QR. Signature policy is a
  setting, manual by default; a facsimile prints only after a manager switches
  it on, and the switch records who and when. Pre-printed stationery is a layout
  option with millimetre offsets.
- **Public check `/cv/{code}`:** a 64-bit random code frozen into each new issue;
  shows status, number, kind, date and a masked name only; TEST issues and
  issues prepared before this release answer "not recognised".
- **Signing tray and class batches** re-lay out frozen snapshots into one print
  file; they never allocate, sign or record. Handover is one idempotent event
  per document with "collected by". Batches run the ordinary command one child
  at a time in the browser (no queue, no worker), so the office can stop.
- **The attention card stays study-only** and now reads the study template, not
  the newest template of any kind.
- **Government returns** become a readiness board, destination heat grid and a
  manual-entry Copilot whose "entered" marks are human evidence only, never
  portal acceptance. "Ask class teachers" links to the existing collection
  rounds; no blocking return field is collectable there yet, so nothing is
  pre-ticked.

## Student documents stay inside the profile — 2 November 2026

The owner requested a simpler Documents section that fits One and a live release.
Use one file list with persistent Add and authenticated Open links, plus the
existing VX sheet for upload. Keep school-issued certificates below attachments.
Tiles consume the narrow drawer; a required-document checklist would need a
school policy that has not been supplied. Keep the six existing types and a
deliberate choice, hide the optional note until wanted, and retain transport
validation/retries. Retrying the same file/metadata keeps the request ID; editing
them starts a new request. Removal confirms the filename and authorizes the
document's actual student. Previewing grants no mutation controls. Routine
implementation/release choices follow CLAUDE rules 5 and 8; no new migration,
financial write, parent dispatch or document completeness claim is introduced.

## Integrated School Office — 1 November 2026

The owner selected contextual actions plus a small School Office register and
authorized implementation/live deployment. Seed the owner-approved Study
Certificate with existing letterhead, manual signature/seal and
`VPPS/STUDY/<session>/0001`; TEST uses a separate clearly marked series. Ordinary
repeat use returns original stored bytes. Intentional new issues/corrections
receive new numbers, with immutable history and exact reserved-issue recovery.
Record download/print requests durably before navigation; neither establishes
physical printing, signature or handover.

Government formats activate only from a school-reviewed current supported
specimen. Unsupported CSV/XLSX shapes use labelled reference workbooks. Returned
files may update only One-owned external registration identity/status after
frozen-preview, current-source and every-row revision checks. Preparing,
submission, partial/full acceptance and rejection remain distinct with evidence.
No second financial or parent-dispatch writer is introduced.

Board authority/attempt/circular/subject requirements are explicit configuration.
Verified registrations freeze exam-period identity/membership so later departure
does not remove an outcome or denominator. New writes require a healthy current
source fence; committed actor/hash-bound receipts replay before new-source checks.
Authority-issued outcomes retain exact strings/statuses and correcting revisions,
separately from internal marks. Calendar reads the same sourced deadline store.

Post go-live immutable School Office tables belong to the legacy quarantine's
untouched K bucket. Its retired first-load relabelling must not rewrite these
records or counters. Current actor/session/TEST guards and private storage remain
the live authority. Only One-owned additive migrations were applied; local
filenames use their actual remote ledger timestamps.

## One student performance — 1 November 2026

Reduce server waterfalls and client work before introducing persistent caches.
The directory overlaps optional field metadata, attendance and portraits;
Student 360 overlaps its already-authorized portrait; the shell streams its
informational bridge status while data readers retain their readiness checks.
Individual marks queries add a pupil filter and bounded paper-ID chunks under
the current cookie/RLS client, while class reports retain the unfiltered mode.

Search controls share an isolated draft provider, so typing does not rebuild the
directory row tree. Use the same query normalization as the server, retain newer
drafts across owned request acknowledgments, and keep the search URL independent
of result page count. Enter is immediate; the debounce is 250 ms; composition and
external navigation cancel obsolete work. A scrolling touch cancels IntentLink's
90 ms full-prefetch timer; keyboard focus remains immediate.

No auth, money or private-photo cache was introduced and no permission grant or
database migration is needed. Existing TEST access refusals are not latency
evidence. Historical migration author/apply timestamp differences remain recorded
history, not migrations to replay. Implementation: `5d54795`.

## Go-live on One's own database — 2026-10-30

The owner approved `docs/cutover/GO-LIVE-2026-27-PLAN.md` on 30 Oct ("implement the plan end to end"), with one constraint stated beforehand: never change the fee app's code or data. The decisions below put that plan into effect. Where the plan left an owner question open, the plan's recommended answer is recorded here and the owner can still overturn it; the plan's question number is given.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-338 | **One's main (production) database is the existing project `wtgxcptmucjerhufzjcf`, filled from read-only snapshots of the live fee database; One is never deployed against `vgqyilgstjvgohrsiwkb`.** The project stops being a dev database: guards key on a `vppsone.deployment` marker instead of the ref, QA, smoke and replica tooling refuse it, and One's test activity is quarantined in place before real data is loaded (plan §0.1). Supersedes D-3 and D-310, and makes `LIVE-MIGRATION-PLAN.md` historical. Where previews and local development go next is open (plan Q19). | The owner named the project on 30 Oct.  The shared-database plan created objects in live and replaced `private.current_staff_role()` and `public.has_permission(text)` there (its M2 step). That is a change to the fee app's data, which the owner has ruled out. |
| D-339 | **`scripts/migrate/*` may read the live fee database directly, read-only by construction**: Postgres sessions with `default_transaction_read_only=on` inside one exported snapshot, and supabase-js clients sealed so that only `select`, Storage `list`/`download`/`createSignedUrl` exist. This amends rule 3 for those scripts only; `replicate-live.mjs` stays the only path for anything else. Output that holds personal data stays outside the repo, in `%USERPROFILE%\.vppsone-migration\`. | A consistent, exact copy of the money needs one snapshot; the masked replica cannot be the go-live data. |
| D-340 | **The test session is the existing `TEST-2026-27`.** Only a real admin (role admin and not a test account) and test accounts can see it; test accounts can see and write nothing else, and a test admin is fenced the same way. Test accounts are flagged by `vppsone.staff_profiles.is_test_account`, which only a real admin can change, and never on themself. Defaults for plan Q2 and Q4: the pill shows for fee readers, coordinators, admin and test accounts; teachers stay on the active session; `2027-28` shows for fee readers. | The owner asked for test data that only admin and test ids can see. A new `TEST` label would fail `fee-rules.ts`'s label shape and bring back the sort trap (D-187). |
| D-341 | **All of One's dev activity moves into `TEST-2026-27` with new ids and no phone numbers. Real master data (subjects, aliases, bells, calendar, policies) is promoted, and the timetable is re-seeded from its fixture.** Every dev profile, real teacher or QA seat, maps to a test account. | `replicate-live.mjs` kept primary keys, so dev ids equal live ids: copying dev rows as they are would attach test marks to real children. Masked `99999…` numbers are real, reachable phones. |
| D-342 | **Online payments: One mints `vp1_` references and refuses every `vpps_` order, intent or capture, on both the payment-link path and the order path, in TypeScript and in SQL.** Receipts posted by One from cutover until written sign-off take suffixes from 5001 (`app_settings.receipt_suffix_floor`). | The two apps share one Razorpay account. The fee app's own ownership check covers links only, and its order path matches on `provider_order_id` alone. The floor keeps fee-tail and rollback receipts from colliding with One's. |
| D-343 | **Every parent WhatsApp from One passes one gate in `modules/whatsapp/data/aisensy.ts`**: a live deployment, `runtime_flags.parent_dispatch`, the message's category in `parent_dispatch_categories`, and an allowlist (`vppsone.message_test_recipients`) for test sessions, test accounts and non-production deployments. It fails closed. Supersedes D-10, D-76, D-270 and D-291 for the four fee categories (receipt, reminder, fee statement, school notice) only, from cutover; other parent categories stay human-pressed. Pay-now templates are a new `_v7` set on `one.vpps.co.in`; One never sends `_v6`. | "Exactly like the fees app" for fee messages, without the ported senders bypassing `parent_dispatch`, which they did by calling AiSensy directly. |
| D-344 | **Staff get WhatsApp from the `staff_notices` outbox only**, with recorded consent, at most 8 a day per person (cover exempt), a 07:00–20:00 IST window (cover exempt), and kill switches in the database, in env and per kind. Weekly summary, missing marks and staff broadcast move from send-by-hand into the outbox. | The owner asked for API messages to staff. D-214's outbox already has the claim rule and "Unknown is never retried". |
| D-345 | **Fee writes in One are off until cutover (`app_settings.fee_writes_enabled`)**, enforced in the database by the money functions and in the app by the fee Supabase server-client wrapper. | In mirror mode the nightly load replaces One's fee tables, so a write would be silently lost, or kept by one system and not the other. |
| D-346 | **There is no separate dev database, ever: `wtgxcptmucjerhufzjcf` is One's production database and its only test bed, and testing happens in it under `TEST-2026-27` by test accounts.** (1) A database marker, `vppsone.deployment.environment` (`dev` today, `production` from the Phase 1 lockdown; unreadable = production), replaces "is this the dev ref?" in every guard. (2) Any deployment that is not Vercel production — Preview and local `next dev` — is **confined**: every signed-in user is treated as a test account, fee writes only for TEST-session students, messages only to the test-recipient allowlist; `isConfinedDeployment()` in `src/platform/deployment/confinement.ts` is the single definition. (3) QA / smoke-lab / day-run / seed tooling is **confined, not banned**: on production it creates or changes only test accounts (`staff_profiles.is_test_account`) and TEST-session data and refuses anything else; `scripts/target-guard.mjs#toolScope` gives each script `read-only`, `test-scoped` or `dev-only` (refuses production). `replicate-live.mjs` refuses production absolutely. (4) TEST-2026-27 receipts use the prefix **`TST`** (a One-owned value in `vppsone.one_owned_fee_settings`), applied in the database on every posting path, desk and online. (5) The mirror never touches test-session rows after the first load. (6) The fees app's local `.env.local` keys for `wtgxcptmucjerhufzjcf` were disabled on 30 Oct with the owner's approval, so running the fees app locally cannot act on One's data. | Owner override T1, 30 Oct: "Testing happens in the same database, under the TEST session." A second project is not available on the free plan (Q1, Q19), and previews and local servers already point at this database, so the only safe default is that they cannot reach real data. Banning QA tooling would leave no way to test in production; confining it keeps testing possible and puts the boundary in the database rather than in an operator's memory. TST stops rehearsal postings from consuming real SVP numbers (Q10). Supersedes plan §0.1's "QA sign-in banned / QA users banned / previews lose their database" bullets |
| D-347 | **The fee writes switch is the owner's, and a production deployment is production even before the marker flips.** (1) `app_settings.fee_writes_enabled` can be inserted, changed or deleted only by a session that did not come through the API (the owner, as postgres, in the SQL editor / MCP); anon, authenticated and the service role are refused (`42501`). (2) The D-345 rule also guards direct writes to the 16 money tables (BEFORE row triggers, every API caller including the service role; the owner's own sessions — migrations, the mirror loader, pg_cron — are exempt) and refuses moving a student or class across the TEST/real boundary while real writes are closed. (3) The app's guards act on `readEffectiveEnvironment()`: production when the marker says so OR the deployment declares `VERCEL_ENV=production` / `VPPSONE_ENV=live`. | Review of PR #141: any admin could flip the switch over PostgREST (`settings:write`, and `has_permission()` is true for every permission of an admin), and RLS let accountants insert payments directly, around the guarded RPCs. (3) Non-terminal fees `online_payment_captures` are not imported at all (their only terminal value, `applied`, would claim a receipt); orders → `abandoned`, intents → `cancelled`, events → `ignored`, outbox → `suppressed`. ## The school's own address — 2026-10-30

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-337 | **The fee app's address is `https://fees.vpps.co.in`; VPPS One links there.** `schoolfees-two.vercel.app` and `vpps-one.vercel.app` keep serving the same deployment and must never redirect. | Every approved WhatsApp template's Pay now button is fixed by Meta at `https://schoolfees-two.vercel.app/pay/{{n}}`, and the Razorpay webhook posts there; moving either needs a template resubmission or a webhook edit, so they stay. The fee app's production `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` and the live Supabase Site URL moved to the new address (old hosts stay in the redirect list). Details: `docs/DOMAIN.md`. |
| D-336 | **VPPS One is served at `https://one.vpps.co.in`, the same Vercel deployment as `vppsone.vercel.app`, which keeps answering with no redirect.** Receipts, QR codes and messages use the new address from the next production build; preview deployments keep linking to themselves. | The owner asked for it on 30 Oct. A redirect would move everyone at once but break the smoke scripts and any open tab, so it waits until staff have moved. DNS is grey-cloud like `fees.vpps.co.in`, because a Cloudflare proxy in front of Vercel stops Vercel renewing its certificate. Supabase Site URL is untouched: sign-in never redirects through Supabase and schoolfees-dev also serves local development. Runbook and rollback: `docs/DOMAIN.md`. |

## One central store and nightly backups in Drive — 2026-10-28

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-335 | **Drive is the document store for every role from 28 Oct 09:10 UTC (`documents_provider = 'gdrive'`), without the office-account pilot.** The owner chose this on 28 Oct over waiting for one upload signed in as QA Office, which Claude cannot make (no password sign-in on the live app; the QA day-run pairing expired 19 Oct). | The admin pilot passed (8 synthetic uploads and opens, checksums equal), the first Drive backup restored 204/204 tables, and the office path differs only in the permission check both roles pass. Backing out is one statement and strands nothing. The first real office upload is the first non-admin evidence and is to be checked (`docs/DRIVE-STORAGE.md`, "Where it stands"). |
| D-334 | **Every role's files go to one administrator-controlled destination, and every record and file is backed up there nightly; Postgres stays the system of record.** The destination is the folder `VPPS One documents` in the Shared Drive "Share", whose only manager is raj@vpps.co.in; files there are owned by the `vpps.co.in` organisation and survive any account's removal. Uploads from admin and office (the only roles with document permissions) go through the backend's service account; nobody connects a Google account. A settings row that cannot be read stops uploads visibly — never a quiet bucket upload — and the old Server Action upload is removed. Changing the destination is refused by a database guard once documents live in Drive unless explicitly confirmed, and every settings change is recorded. A nightly `drive-backup` job writes into `backups/`: sealed copies of every object in the four buckets that hold people's files, the approved copy-only move of bucket documents into Drive (originals kept), and a sealed snapshot of every `vppsone` and fee table with a plaintext manifest. Auth accounts are not exported. Retention: keep everything until the owner decides. | Owner's brief and answers, 28 Oct: one destination under raj@vpps.co.in, no teacher-owned storage, backups of records and files in the same destination; keep the "Share" folder (not My Drive, which would need a stored refresh token and goes with the account); keep every backup for now; copy the 11 synthetic bucket documents copy-only. A second folder in the same drive is not independent of losing that drive: `docs/DRIVE-STORAGE.md` says what still stands outside it. |

## Student documents in Google Drive — 2026-10-27

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-333 | **Student documents can live in a dedicated school Shared Drive; the row stays the index, and Drive is off until approved.** `student_documents` gains `storage_provider`, `drive_file_id`, `content_sha256`, `content_version`, `upload_state`, `client_request_id`, `removed_at/by` and backup columns; `file_storage_settings` (one server-only row of identifiers) says where new uploads go: `supabase` by default, a pilot list, then `gdrive`. The app authenticates with its Vercel OIDC token through Workload Identity Federation to a service account that is a **Contributor** on one Shared Drive — no key anywhere. Uploads arrive in 3.75 MiB chunks through VPPS One (Vercel's 4.5 MB body cap), magic bytes checked before Drive sees a byte, Drive's own sha256 compared at the end; documents stream back through `/documents/<id>` with `private, no-store`. Removing a Drive document hides it; the app never deletes from Drive. A nightly `document-backup` job seals each Drive document (AES-256-GCM, key wrapped RSA-OAEP for the school's offline recovery key; the app holds only the public half) into the Supabase bucket `vppsone-drive-backups`, which year-end archives include. Profile photographs, fee buckets and generated PDFs stay as they are. `/documents/<id>` now reads the row as the person asking, closing a cross-class read by id. | Owner's brief, 27 Oct: Drive as the private store for student documents, least privilege, no per-user Google sign-in, files private, real backup before migration, nothing switched on without the specific approvals. The org forbids service-account keys, so keyless federation is the only option without an org-policy change. A second folder in the same Google account is not a backup. `docs/DRIVE-STORAGE.md` has the setup, the switch, recovery, limits and what is still unmeasured. |

## Smoke Lab round 2 — 2026-10-27

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-331 | **A Smoke Lab seat gets at most 5 AI helper drafts a day** (`SMOKE_ASSIST_DAILY_CAP` in `src/modules/smoke/domain/limits.ts`, applied by `dailyCapFor` in `runAssistTask`); the admin's cap still wins when lower. The agents' rules quote the same constant, and a refusal at the cap is expected, not a finding. | The helper is on for the test deployment with a $25 month and 60 drafts a person a day. A 28-seat run at that cap could spend the month in one afternoon and switch the helper off for the school's own testers. Five drafts tries every button a role is offered; worst case about $4 a run. A brief rule alone is not a cap: an agent in a loop does not read rules. |
| D-332 | **Round 2 is prepared and not started.** The plan lives in the repository (`docs/smoke/plans/school-day.json`: 28 seats, a persona each, ten waves of at most three browsers with notes on what each wave checks from the earlier ones), checked by `checkPlan` (pure, tested against the committed file). `npm run smoke:ready` is read-only: the plan, the lab switched on at `APP_ORIGIN` (401 from `/api/lab/brief`, not 404), and with the dev key the seats, open runs and the AI month. The school day gains the AI helper: brief, cover plan, call scripts, results summary, note helper, notes in short, remark drafts, notice writer, Ask. | The 20 Oct plan lived only in a git-ignored folder on another machine, so the next run would have started from nothing. A plan holds no pass, so it can be committed; the kit, which does, still cannot. The owner asked for an environment ready to run, not a run. |

## AI helper — 2026-10-27

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-330 | **The AI helper on a phone.** Below 900px every AI button is a full-width 48px row whose words wrap (the kit's `.btn` is `nowrap` at a fixed height, which ran long labels off a 360px screen); a suggestion's buttons stack with the main one across the full width. On a touch screen an AI button closes the keyboard first and the suggestion scrolls itself into view above the bottom bar. Read-only suggestions (summaries, call script, brief, cover plan, results) get Copy — with a fallback where `navigator.clipboard` is missing — and, where the phone has one, the system Share sheet; both count as "kept" once and leave the card open, because a call script is read during the call. With no signal the buttons are disabled and say they need the internet, and nothing is sent. The admission form offers "Take a photo" and "Choose from gallery" (a form already photographed on a parent's phone). Styles live in `src/vx/assist/assist.css`, weighted above the kit's 44px coarse-pointer rule. `tests/mobile-ui/assist.html` draws every AI piece with canned answers for checks at 375px and 1280px | Owner, 27 Oct: "optimize the AI helper updates for mobile users". Checked in the phone harness at 375 px: 14 AI buttons all 48px tall, no horizontal scroll, Hindi labels wrap, offline state shown; at 1280 px the buttons stay 36px pills. Large text already reaches the cards through `.vx-large .vx-main { zoom }` |
| D-328 | **Numbers come from the app, words from the AI.** Call scripts and the Today brief write every amount, date, count, percentage and name as a `<<slot>>` from a list the screen offers; the screen fills the slots with its own figures (shown in bold). A line with a slot that was not offered, or with any digit the model wrote itself, is dropped, and a script without a clean opening is no draft at all (`domain/facts.ts`). For one family's call, **only slot names and plain situation phrases travel** — the parent's name, the children's names, the amount and the dates never leave the browser. The Today brief sends school-wide figures (no child or family) so the model can reason about them, and still must cite them as slots. Note summaries may repeat a number only if it appears in the notes or figures they were given | The second round (owner, 27 Oct: "build more suggested ideas") puts AI next to money and attendance. A model that restates "₹4,500" can restate it wrong; a model that never writes a digit cannot |
| D-329 | Second-round features: **Notes in short** (a child's notes as points and an opening line before a parent meeting, on the teacher's child view and Student 360; a class's last seven days of notes as themes, who needs a follow-up and who deserves praise, on the notes board and the teacher's notes) — Haiku 5.5; **Call scripts** on the dues call sheet and the attendance call sheet — Haiku 5.5; **Template drafts** on the new WhatsApp template sheet, using only `{parent} {student} {class} {date} {amount}` — Haiku 5.5; **Today brief** on the admin/office Today screen — Sonnet 5.5. Health and family notes (`SENSITIVE_TAGS`) are never sent; the class digest sends children as C1, C2 with every name in the notes masked. Nothing is saved: summaries and scripts are read or copied, a template is copied into the sheet and still sent for approval by the person | The notes board already treats health and family notes as sensitive. The brief is on Sonnet because the principal reads it; the others are read by the person who asked |
| D-324 | VPPS One has an optional AI helper that uses the school's Claude Console key (`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, server only). It **drafts and never decides**: every result lands in a field the person still edits and saves through that screen's own action and checks, or is just read (a cover plan, a results summary). Nothing is saved, queued or sent by AI. Without the key, or with the admin's switch off, every AI button is absent and every screen is exactly what it was. Features: note helper, report-card remarks, cover-period plan, notice/reply writer, paper-form reading on the admission wizard, spreadsheet column matching, results summary, Ask in the search box, translate. The worksheet builder was left out at the owner's request | PLAN §9 allows AI as "suggestions with review only" and §1 forbids requiring it. The owner asked (27 Oct) to build all the brainstormed uses except the worksheet builder, on their Claude Console. Spending is authorised by that request; the admin's monthly budget (default $25) and per-person daily cap (default 60) bound it |
| D-325 | Two models, chosen by the task and never shown as a choice: **Claude Haiku 5.5** for short rewrites, translation, matching, cover plans and Ask; **Claude Sonnet 5.5** where a parent or the principal reads the result or a photo is read (remarks, results summary, admission form). Every task runs at effort `low`, uses structured outputs (zod) and sends no `temperature`. Sonnet requests opt into the server-side refusal fallback (`fallbacks: "default"`); Haiku has none, so a decline there shows "write it yourself" | Haiku 5.5 is $0.10/$0.50 per million tokens and a note rewrite costs about $0.0003; Sonnet 5.5 is $2/$10. The brainstorm's estimate for all features was ~$12 a month, ~$21 in a report-card month. Sampling parameters return 400 on these models; low effort is the recommended level for short writing and extraction |
| D-326 | What leaves the school is minimised in code, not by instruction: phone numbers, Aadhaar-like numbers, emails and every name the screen knows are replaced by `[[N1]]`-style placeholders before sending and put back after (`domain/redact.ts`); a draft that comes back with a placeholder the model invented is dropped. Remarks and results send no names at all — children are `C1`, `C2` with their figures. Column matching sends headings only. The admission form photo is the one exception and says so on screen. `vppsone.assist_log` keeps only metadata (who, feature, model, tokens, cost, kept/edited/discarded), never a prompt or an answer | Notes and remarks are about children. A screen can know the child's name; it cannot know every name typed into a note, so the features that write about many children are built so there is no name to send |
| D-327 | One door: `POST /api/assist` runs a task, `PATCH` records the decision. Order of checks: key present → the person already has the permission the job needs by hand (`FEATURE_PERMISSIONS`) → admin switch and per-feature switch → month's spend under budget and person under the daily cap (one RPC; unreadable counts as spent) → input validated by the task's zod schema → request. Settings → AI helper (admin) holds the switch, budget, cap, per-feature switches and this month's use and kept-rate. The key is never entered in the app | A server action per screen would have spread the checks over nine files; one route makes them impossible to skip. The kept/edited/discarded rate is how the school learns which features are worth their cost |

## Workspace tidy — 2026-10-26

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-323 | The 26 single-purpose Playwright configs live in `tests/playwright/`. Every path in them is written from the repository root through `fromRoot()`/`ROOT` (`tests/playwright/root.ts`), and each `webServer` runs with `cwd: ROOT`. Configs that used Playwright's default output now name `test-results/` at the root, so the CI artifact uploads are unchanged. The workflows still pass bare file names in their matrices, because artifact names cannot hold `/` | The repo root held 26 configs beside the real project files. Before and after the move, `playwright test --list --reporter=json` gave the same testDir, outputDir, snapshotDir, webServer command and test count for every config (fee-collector-dev refuses to load without its posting signals both times, by design) |

From the 19 October fix brief (B01–B16, R01, U01–U05; ledger in `docs/evidence/assessment-fixes-2026-10-19.md`). No schema, grant or policy change; not merged or deployed.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-297 | One marks progress contract: eligible = the paper's roster by the exam's dates; saved = a value (zero counts), absent or exempt; a cleared cell is blank. Every surface (teacher Today, the sheet, the tracker, readiness) counts through `exams/domain/progress.ts` / `trackerFor`, and shows unsaved device edits apart | B01/B02: Today counted blank rows over every "enrolled" row and showed 5/8 where the sheet showed 3/8 |
| D-298 | Draft and correction saves send and validate only the cells that changed; a historical mark that breaks the paper's step is flagged, never rounded or rewritten; a final submit is checked against the whole saved column on the server (`submitBlocker`) and lock refuses invalid or incomplete papers | B03/B04: 16–24 replica half marks on whole-mark papers blocked any new valid mark. The exam office's rounding rule (PROGRESS 14 Oct) is still theirs to choose |
| D-299 | Undo of a marks submission is offered from the server's remaining window, counted on the phone's own clock; the server's 10-minute refusal is unchanged | B05: old receipts promised Undo after it had expired |
| D-300 | The balance after a payment is read from the live ledger (`balanceSource: "live"`, `readAt`) and shown as "Balance now" beside the receipt's immutable "still owed at this receipt"; an empty live read counts as nothing owed only when the receipt agrees; Retry re-reads and can never post. The financial-view drain runs after the answer, for dashboards. Revises D-271 | B07: the drain returns false under `skip locked` while the fee action's own drain holds it, and the refresh is concurrent, so "drain then read" showed ₹7,100 for ₹7,099 |
| D-301 | A receipt's URL may open only a sheet its state allows (`reverse-sheet.ts`); any other is replaced. The admin who decides a waiting reversal request still opens the sheet. Decided desk requests stay in My requests | B08 |
| D-302 | A support plan made from an Only me note starts with an empty goal, says who will read it, and takes the note's words only through a deliberate, previewed "Use words from the note"; the server checks the source note as the writer may read it (same child, same session), refuses unshared private words and any parents' message; plans that still carry private words, or come from an Only me health/family note, are their author's alone, and cannot be overwritten by someone they are hidden from. The RLS hardening for direct REST reads is proposed, not applied | R01 was a source-level path, not an observed exposure: DEV had 0 plans from its 2 private notes |
| D-303 | A note belongs to the school day it was written on in IST (`noteSchoolDay`), and "you" is decided by staff id, never by role or name | B09 |
| D-304 | Leave periods are resolved once (`leavePeriods`/`leaveSpanFor` in `timetable/domain/timetable.ts`), through a read-only `previewLeaveVx`, and match the approval's `spanPeriods`; a half day is asked one day at a time; one-period leave stays unsupported rather than broadened | B11: Today said seven periods, More said three, the approval covered four |
| D-305 | Leave asked and not decided is an item in Needs you (`substitutions:manage`), decided where it always was, on the timetable's leave tab | Brief §02: the admin saw leave only by opening that tab |
| D-306 | "Periods a week" means timetable slots everywhere; the planned per-class figure is labelled "Planned: N class-periods a week" | B12: 92 vs 44 |
| D-311 | AI agents smoke-test the app through a Smoke Lab: 14 `smoke.*@qa.vpps.local` accounts (six attached to real class-teacher profiles on the test data), signed in only with an admin-issued pass (256 bits, shown once, stored hashed, 24 h at most, revocable), and a smoke session is honoured only while its pass and run are live (middleware → `smoke_access_allowed()`) | Owner asked 20 Oct for passwordless role logins so agents can run whole school days on the deployed app and locally; chose deployed+local, a new pool, findings in the app. The revoked overnight launcher (PR #101) is not revived; this is a separate, simpler design on the same server-side magic-link sign-in as `/auth/confirm` |
| D-312 | The Smoke Lab is on only while the app points at the test database and is not declared live (`smokeLabEnabled`), and its migration is a D-skip for the live cutover | Same gate as the keyed test sign-in, without needing `QA_LOGIN_KEY`: the pass is the secret |
| D-316 | A green, "done" or "saved" state is drawn only from server-confirmed data, and names what is left ("3 of 14 sent · 11 blank", "0 of 3 periods have a teacher"); nothing to count is grey, never a green 0/0 | The 20 Oct school day found this one mistake in a dozen places (cover ring, leave toast, marks list, saved register card, offline banner). Fixes: `docs/evidence/smoke-school-day-2026-10-21-fixes.md` |
| D-317 | Language: a Hindi-only reader gets no English sub-lines; one three-way control (English / हिंदी / both) everywhere; Hindi dates and times from fixed tables ("रात 10:59 बजे"), never Intl hi-IN (Node and browsers differ); a banned-words test keeps draft/submit/पुष्टि/प्रविष्टि and raw codes out of Hindi strings, now at zero | Hindi-reading teachers could not read half of each screen and lost the "both" setting with one tap |
| D-318 | Fees: "pending today" means due before today (or last session's balance); due today is its own state. One rule (`fees/domain/due-rule.ts`) on the Desk, Dues, Reports, Accounts and Students; a receipt's "fees still owed" counts this session's money net of reversals | Reports said ₹52.7 L pending where Dues said ₹26.6 L, and a receipt said ₹399 owed for a child owing ₹10,399. The fee app has the same receipt bug; this departs from it on purpose |
| D-319 | Report cards follow the Grace screen's rule paper by paper (below the pass mark after grace, or missed and not excused, is not passed) as well as the overall pass %; list and card use one totals function and print exam weights. **Needs Janmejay's confirmation**, and issued cards keep their old result until reissued | A card said "Pass" for a child with 0 in Maths whom Grace listed as short. No document states the school's pass rule |
| D-320 | Parent WhatsApp on a register is ON only for the first save of today's register, OFF for past days and corrections, and the teacher's choice sticks; a correction never queues a parent again after the first message went | A typed note toggled the switch on, and corrections re-queued messages already sent. Confirm the same-day ON default with Janmejay |
| D-321 | `vppsone.assign_late_cover` (DEV `20261021033411`, goes live at cutover): an admin with substitutions:manage can send a free teacher into a lesson that is running now with no teacher, under the cover day's lock; the lab clock still never changes writes | The cover engine treats a begun period as history, so a period nobody covered stayed empty all day |
| D-322 | Open for the owner: whether reversals count on the receipt's day (kept) or the day they are made; which coordinator gets teachers' follow-ups when there are two; whether Half yearly papers allow Exempt (the exam office can now switch it per paper); whether the accountant opens Collection | Each changes school policy or the books; the code does the safe thing until decided |
| D-315 | The smoke pool holds 20 teachers: class teachers first, then subject teachers, then new profiles with nothing assigned. Agents work through `scripts/smoke/session.mjs`, a persistent seat with screenshots, an accessibility outline, and tap-target and overflow checks. A run opens from a plan file through the `/smoke` console (`open-run.mjs`) | The owner asked for a whole school day with an admin, both coordinators, an accountant and 20 teachers, seen from a non-technical teacher's side. Waves of at most three browsers (laptop limit), each handed what the previous one saved for cross-checks; a developer agent groups findings by root cause (`docs/evidence/smoke-school-day-2026-10-20.md`) |
| D-314 | After an independent security review: a smoke account can never hold `staff:manage` or `settings:manage` (or the fee app's `staff:manage`), whatever its role; the database bans every pool account with no live pass and deletes its sessions (`smoke_enforce()`, on every open/close/revoke and every 2 min by pg_cron); the gate is asked in the middleware *and* in `getCurrentStaff`; a "no" signs out every session; accounts carry `app_metadata.smoke_lab`; the API takes the pass only from the `Authorization` header | Review found the admin seat could create a password account or change roles and outlive its run (High), and an agent's tokens kept refreshing after its run ended if it never touched the app (High). The ban uses a far-future date, not `'infinity'`: Auth cannot read `'infinity'` and listing users failed for the whole test project until `20261020110015` fixed it |
| D-313 | Agents file findings into the app (`/api/lab/findings`, deduplicated by role, screen and folded title; repeats counted), the admin triages on `/smoke` and exports Markdown for fix PRs; a scripted sweep files the obvious first | Findings in one place both people and developer agents can work from; the lab's own pages are a test tool shown only to the admin and to agents, so `/lab/ended` is English-only |
| D-310 | The live cutover applies the repository migration files one per call, in filename order, in four phases: M1 the `vppsone` schema (QA fixture runs, QA history cleanup and the overnight QA launcher never go to live), M2 the two fee-side function replacements and the defaulter writer with the owner's approval, M3 schema exposure, M4 first admin and settings. M2 is rolled back before anything touches `vppsone` | `docs/cutover/LIVE-MIGRATION-PLAN.md`, reviewed independently. `CUTOVER.md`'s "nothing touches `public`" was wrong; 67 of 79 files match what DEV ran and the other 12 differ only in wording. Prepared, not run (rule 8) |

## Shared teacher work acceptance - 2026-10-16

- Final review corrections preserve the original task credential through the atomic collection writer and recheck management authority after every task/request lock wait. Installed unmanaged requests use the same transactional proposal writer. Scalar drafts are actor/capability/request-generation/session/roster/field scoped; signed-in legacy photos use an explicit authenticated assigned-request generation. These tighten the existing scope without new SQL objects, grants or migration data effects. The parent confirms the revised three-body packet is covered by the existing user approval.
- Patch only sharp 0.35.5 for the concrete new upload finding and reject unsupported container signatures before decoding. The user's priority-release scope defers baseline Next/eslint/source-map-js remediation. Final full Vitest/build/bundle gates run on mandatory corrected-head CI concurrently with scoped review after local focused, SQL/PG, affected-browser and static checks; no merge before green. [Six controller rulings](evidence/sampark-workflows/rulings.md).
- Teacher collection proposals require an active administrator whether entered through a managed task or an older `/r` request. The database trigger rejects office decisions inside the existing canonical transaction, rolling back its record/history/command writes; the review action and UI use the same boundary. Unrelated direct-office record and photo policy remains unchanged. The regression uses the actual canonical writer, not a review mock.
- Per the user's 10:31 UTC steering, external AiSensy campaign/template setup remains pending in existing Integrations. Local message-template rows establish only local contracts. Readiness follows the existing task-delivery availability gate; reviewed manual task links remain available without claiming delivery. Six initial/reminder language contracts are retained for later authorized setup. Provider configuration is not a release blocker for the other approved work.
- An outstanding academic command is described as school save unconfirmed. Recovery keeps the original command across reload; it cannot claim that the school has not saved it. Attributed not-sent provider reconciliation clears local uncertainty so the user can immediately review a new send. Queued means queued, not sent.
- Apply the three exact approved `vppsone` migrations before releasing dependent flows. Prefer code rollback while retaining additive tasks, proposals, private images and history; destructive schema reversal needs its own data-preserving plan. [Exact objects, grants, hashes and rollback](evidence/sampark-workflows/migration-approval.md), [local acceptance and limitations](evidence/sampark-workflows/task-4-report.md). Final review, approval, CI and deployment are separate controller gates.

Raj sent phone screenshots after #98: Student360's "Change photograph" floated between the header and the figures, its two teacher requests sat as loose pills under the tabs, and Needs you drew Assign work, Review photographs and an empty "To assign 0" box above its own title. The same pattern was on Students, the staff profile, the teacher's class and profile screens, and Preferences.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-295 | A control added to an existing screen goes where that screen already keeps that kind of action, in the vx kit: a photograph is changed from a camera badge on the portrait ring (and the ⋯ menu); asking a teacher is a ⋯ row on a phone and an "Ask a teacher" button in Student360's desk action row; Needs you's Assign work and Review photographs are top-bar buttons on a desk, and teacher work is one card below the queue; the missing-details filter is a select in the Students toolbar and a section of the phone Filter sheet, with Assign work on a banner for its matches and in the bulk bar for picked rows. Editors open in sheets, not inline | Each had been added as a Tailwind pill outside the vx layout (two above a page title), so it looked bolted on and pushed content down. Reachability is unchanged: every action is still one or two taps from where it was |
| D-296 | Components shown inside vx screens resolve text with vx's `lead()`; the photo editor does so only when opened as a sheet | For a "both" reader vx leads with English, `primary()` with Hindi, so the assignment and photo sheets switched language mid-page. The teachers' link pages keep `primary()` |

## Phone: student filters and stepwise collection — 2026-10-16

Raj asked, on a phone, for filters and other ways to see the Students list without making it complicated, and for collecting a fee to stop needing scrolling: details, then amount, then date and save. Screens: UI-94 and UI-95 in `docs/ui/DECISIONS.md`. After using it he asked for the class list to scroll, the keyboard to open on its own and for more speed (UI-96, D-362; numbered when it merged, after main's D-295–D-361).

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-292 | A fee-list scope longer than 200 ids is paged in code: its ids and sort keys are read in 200-id chunks under the page query's own predicates, ordered as `applyStudentSort` orders them, and only the page's ids reach the page query; the count is the scope's own (`pageOfLargeScope` in `students-fees/data/queries.ts`, both the list and the identity pass) | `getStudentsPage` put every matching id in one `.in()`. On dev in 2026-27 "Part paid" is 390 children, "Pending" 316 and "On bus" 302, against a URL limit near 300 (rule 3b): the new filters would have hung for eight seconds and read as "could not read". Ported code, changed here only; the fee app keeps the bug |
| D-293 | Students filters are the fee app's own saved views (`SEGMENT_OF` in `students-fees/vx/admin-students.ts`), combined as the fee app combines them: either within a group (Pending or Paid up), both across groups (Pending and on a bus). The four quick chips each still show just their group; combinations come only from the phone's Filter sheet and show as chips with their own × | One definition of each set, so a chip's count and the list it opens agree. Keeping the quick chips single means nobody's desk habit changes |
| D-294 | On a phone, leaving the amount step needs an amount the ledger can take and, for a waiver, a reason; date problems stay on the date step. An unfinished save holds its child on the last step (its sheet and banner as before), and holds another child's Next | Step 3 never opens on a problem only step 2 can fix; the recovery rules of D-271/D-275 are unchanged because the review sheet, the save and the attempt store are untouched |
| D-362 | A child's desk load starts the installment ledger and the session's receipts beside the fee summary, not after it; the ledger is read again if it lacks a row the summary has (dues prepared during that same load). A row in the class list starts the child's load when the finger lands on it, and the tap uses it if it follows within 10 s | The load was five round trips in a row; two of them needed only the child. The re-read keeps D-271's rule that the desk never offers less than the ledger will take. A press that turns into a scroll costs one unused read |
| D-363 | **Scheduled jobs ping an outside watcher, and One's server errors go to Sentry project `vppsone-staging`.** (1) A scheduled run that ends `ok` or `skipped` ("already done today" still proves the scheduler fired) GETs `HEARTBEAT_URL_<JOB>` (`drive-backup` → `HEARTBEAT_URL_DRIVE_BACKUP`); `failed` and `busy` stay silent so the watcher alarms. Unset = no ping; 3 s timeout; a failed ping is a warning, never the job's failure. Manual runs from the console do not ping. The URL is any ping-a-URL check-in (Healthchecks.io's free tier since 3 Nov 2026 — the Student Pack's Honeybadger offer would not redeem), so leaving that provider is an env change, not a code change. (2) `SENTRY_DSN` for One Production is the Default key of `vppsone-staging` in Sentry org `veer-patta-school-q6` — One-owned, never the fees project, as D-359 requires; the name says staging because it was made before D-338. No SDK, no new dependency. | Owner, 3 Nov 2026: use Student Pack offers only where leaving them later costs nothing. tests/heartbeat.test.ts. |

## A whole school day, run by agents — 2026-10-14

Six role agents ran 14 Oct as the school's staff; five developer agents fixed what they found (PRs 87–91); two verifiers re-ran it and #92 fixed the rest. Account: `docs/evidence/dayrun-2026-10-14.md`.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-274 | The Payment Desk refuses a counter concession (UI removed, both server actions refuse > 0); the late-fee waiver stays | `post_student_payment_with_adjustments` writes a `discount` row to `public.receipt_adjustments`, but `v_workbook_installment_balances` reads `payment_adjustments` and never `receipt_adjustments`: the receipt printed "Concession ₹500" and the ledger still asked for it. The fee app has the same code. We may not alter fee views, so the field goes until the fee side is fixed; the waiver path (`student_late_fee_waivers`) was checked on dev and is honoured. Needs a school/fee-app decision to re-enable |
| D-275 | A desk save answers with the receipt number as soon as the RPC returns; the receipt and balance follow from `settleDeskSaveAction`, which still drains the balance views first | Keeps D-271 (no stale balance on a receipt) without the 12 s – 2.5 min "Saving…" the cashier saw with the parent's cash in hand |
| D-276 | Receipts, Dues and the accountant's Today read fee rows with the fee service-role client, after their own `fees:view` check | Fee RLS keys on `public.users.role`; the office account has no such row and read "₹0 · 0 receipts" with no error. Read-only paths only; writes keep the cookie client and D-179's authorisation |
| D-277 | Posting into a day already closed on Receipts warns on the review sheet; it does not block | Blocking needs fee RPC changes, and admins back-date on purpose; Receipts still flags receipts posted after the close |
| D-278 | "The current exam" is picked by dates (`pickCurrentExam`): the open, dated exam with papers that began most recently; an undated exam only when nothing else exists. A teacher's marks tab lists every open exam's papers, overdue first (`openExamsForMarks`) | `!startsOn` was read as "started long ago", so a dateless test exam took over the exam office, results and every teacher's marks tab, and Half-yearly was hidden. Rule 3a: never by label |
| D-279 | Report-card columns come from every subject's exams; an empty, incomplete or unschemed card cannot be issued (button and both server actions) | The header was built from the first subject, so FA1 marks sat under SA1 on screen, print and PDF |
| D-280 | "Late" means behind the expected pace, and after the due date every unsubmitted paper is late; a paper with no subject teacher belongs to the class teacher everywhere | Three screens showed 86 %, 64 % and 85 % and late counts of 17 and 15 for the same exam |
| D-281 | Per-request React `cache()` for the admin board and Needs you; cross-request `sharedCache` (300 s) for calendar, published timetable, bells, subjects, timetable day names and large text — permission checks outside, every tag expired by the writes that change it. No cross-request cache of anything read with the user's cookie client | Admin Today made ~70 PostgREST calls (board 3×, inbox 2×); the cookie-client-in-cache trap is the one that broke the Payment Desk before |
| D-282 | The shell's dues badge has its own five-minute count and is not refreshed by each posting | It re-scanned `v_workbook_student_financials` (~480 ms) after every receipt; all money figures still refresh on posting |
| D-283 | A failed script chunk reloads the page once (not again within a minute, never without storage), then shows a bilingual error with Reload | On a slow link a 27 KB chunk timeout replaced the page with Next's English crash screen |
| D-284 | Marks entry moves to the next child only when the mark is finished: never after one digit on a paper out of 20 or more, never while a half/quarter-mark paper could still take a point; a stray point after a move goes back to its box; invalid input is refused, not stripped | "95" on an 80-mark paper saved 9 and 5; "72.5" saved 72 and .5; "-5" saved 5 |
| D-285 | Teacher-facing Hindi is gender-neutral (respectful plural: ले लेंगे, नहीं ले पाएँगे); nothing is pre-selected in a reason list; the leave sheet starts empty | Male teachers read "ले रही हैं"; the office received reasons nobody chose; tapping Friday also requested today |
| D-286 | The language button labelled EN stores `en`; "English with Hindi" stays in Preferences | It stored `both`, so a teacher could not reach English alone |
| D-287 | Cover candidates on screen are teaching staff (or anyone the published timetable gives a lesson); same-named staff carry their short name. The server command still accepts a manual pick of anyone active (D-213) | Office, admin, accountant and view-only logins were offered as "Best match" |
| D-288 | A cover save whose answer was lost is resolved by re-reading the command's outcome, on load and straight after the timeout: landed → banner clears; not landed → "Not saved · Try again" with the same command id | The banner survived reloads and greyed out every Assign. Idempotency (D-212) and the scoped locks are unchanged |
| D-289 | Periods that had already begun when a teacher is marked away are listed as "Already begun · not covered"; they are computed for display, no duty rows | The screen said "0 periods · No cover needed" for a teacher with seven periods |
| D-290 | The students list's "Not due yet" is `outstanding − overdue`; the late fee is not subtracted again | `outstanding_amount` is fees only (equal to the installments' pending fees for all 593 children on dev); a new admission showed ₹2,000 short |
| D-291 | The accountant's Today makes no promise of automatic reminders and has no pause switch | No job sends fee reminders (vercel.json has none) and nothing reads `acc_reminder_pauses`; the card said "at 4:00 pm · On for today" |

## Whole-student improvement notes — 2026-10-11

The 11 October notes ("VPPS One: practical improvements for the whole student") chose approach 2: rebalance the existing modules around roles and students, no new suite, no student score. PRs 73 and 74.

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-249 | The payment form always gets its column's full width; "Where it goes" sits beside it only from a 700px column | Two nested grids (a 392px directory, a fixed 312px preview) left the form as the only flexible track: ~104px at a 900px container, ~228px at an 1100px window with the sidebar. Measured after: 541px at 1100px (preview below), 553px beside a 312px preview at 1440px |
| D-250 | Review is a step in the right pane, not a sheet, and its button says "Confirm and save payment" | The notes asked for a genuine review screen where nothing is saved yet and Back keeps entries. Back was checked to keep ₹7,000 |
| D-251 | A save with no answer is "could not confirm", never "nothing was posted"; the desk and the server both ask the database by `client_request_id` before another try | The desk caught every thrown save as "Not saved. Nothing was posted." although a lost response may follow a posted payment, and the classic action throws "Payment saved, but receipt details are missing" after posting. The same request id is kept until a save is confirmed, so a retry returns the earlier receipt instead of writing a second (`receipts_student_client_request_id_unique`) |
| D-252 | Overdue (due before today), Due today and Upcoming are separate figures that add up to "Remaining this year"; Student 360 and the Students list say "Overdue" and "Due today & upcoming" | The desk counted an installment due today as "Not due yet". The fee app's "pending today" is installments whose status is overdue, so the honest word is Overdue. Year Clear keeps the fee app's `isYearCleared` rule |
| D-253 | The Students list opens in a School view for everyone; Fees is a view for `fees:view`, remembered per person in a cookie tied to the staff id | The list was finance-led for every reader. A cookie lets the server render the right view first; tying it to the staff id stops a shared office computer handing one person's choice to the next. `staff_ui_preferences` had no column for it, and a migration was not worth one preference |
| D-254 | A row shows at most three factual badges, then "+N details"; concessions never headline the School view, money badges never reach a reader without `fees:view` | The notes: facts with a period and source, never "weak" or a risk score; family concession reasons are office context, not a teacher-facing label |
| D-255 | An incomplete term is labelled "Provisional · N paper(s) not entered" beside its percentage and grade | `totalsFor()` leaves unentered papers out of both halves, so the percentage is of the entered papers only. Showing it as a final aggregate overstated it; the label says what it is without hiding the figure |
| D-256 | `/notes?student=` lists only that child's notes; all-school is a deliberate link | `student` only set the detail pane, so a profile's "All notes" opened the whole school's list. Scoping narrows what the reader could already see and widens nothing |
| D-257 | The old notes reader names its author embed | Notes gained two more foreign keys to `staff_profiles` on 10 October, which made `author:staff_profiles(...)` ambiguous and emptied the profile's Notes tab. The embed test now also checks `!inner` embeds |
| D-258 | The admin Today leads with "Scheduled now", then teaching exceptions, support, a compact approvals count and only then money | The notes: order Today around the school day. "Scheduled now" is from the published timetable with today's cover applied and says it is not a record of delivery. Shared, parallel and combined slots (ELGA) show once with every teacher |
| D-259 | (Superseded by D-261 on 2026-10-11.) The fee collector is not given `fees:collect` | Posting needs the fee app's `payments:write`, which the fee database refuses to `fee_collector` (D-191). Granting `fees:collect` alone would show a Payment Desk where every save fails, and would open day close and the accounts book. A product decision for Janmejay |
| D-260 | (Superseded on 2026-10-11 by D-262, D-263 and D-265, built with defaults.) Not built from the notes, on purpose: a marks finalisation change, bulk student-data approval, family-contact outcome logging | Marks already distinguish blank, value, absent and exempt, and the exam office reopens; student-data review already shows previous → new value, sender and staleness (D-164). The notes list these as decisions to make before design (§10), so they wait for those answers |
| D-261 | Fee collectors post payments at the Payment Desk and print the receipt; the back office moves to a new `fees:accounts` (accountant, admin) | Janmejay, in the thread on 11 Oct 2026: "Yes fee collector ls can post payment". Supersedes D-259. VPPS One gives `fee_collector` `fees:collect`; the fee vocabulary gives it `payments:write` and `receipts:print`, and nothing else that moves money (no undo, reversal, waiver or bulk). `fees:accounts` now gates closing the day, asking for a reversal, the bank books, refunds, bulk entry and pausing reminders. Migration `20261011092957`. In the **schoolfees-dev** fee database `public.has_permission` now grants the two fee permissions to `fee_collector`. **The live fee database does not have this grant: run `docs/cutover/fee-collector-posts-payments.sql` there at the cutover, or a fee collector's post will be refused** |
| D-262 | An admin finalises (locks) and reopens a class's marks; exam coordinators no longer lock | Notes §10.1 asked who finalises. Default chosen until the school answers: the admin, for both. One list in `src/vx/screens/exams/domain/finalise.ts` (`MARKS_FINALISERS`, `MARKS_REOPENERS`); add `"exam_coordinator"` there to hand the lock back. Subject teachers still edit their paper until they submit it, and a submitted paper can still be handed back. An incomplete term shows "Provisional · N papers not entered" beside the figure (D-256) |
| D-263 | Bulk approval of student-data changes covers only the rows the reviewer ticks, with each field's old → new value in the list; nothing pre-ticked, no unexplained "Approve all" | Notes §7. A row whose stored value changed since the request (conflict) or that a newer request replaced (superseded) cannot be ticked and is listed with the reason, to be decided one at a time where "replace the newer value" is offered. Every tick goes through the same `decideSubmissionsAction` with one command id per press, so stale-revision and replay rules hold row by row (`bulkEligibility` in `modules/collection/domain/review.ts`) |
| D-264 | Health and family notes (tags `health`, `family`; older kinds `health`, `parent`) are read only by their writer, the follow-up owner, admins and the class's current class teacher; other support notes by the child's teachers and admins | Notes §9/§10.5. One rule in TypeScript (`canSeeNote`, `isSensitiveNote`) and the same in RLS (`notes_select_scoped`, migration `20261011090858`). Coordinators who read every class no longer see health/family notes unless they hold the class. Plans built from such a note follow it. The note sheet's "Who can read it" says "Admin and class teacher" for these tags. 6 of 33 dev notes are sensitive |
| D-265 | Family contact is recorded as what happened — tried, spoke, follow-up agreed (with the agreed date) — by the class's current class teacher or a school-wide reader | Notes §6: "drafting a message does not mean it was sent". A new `contact` kind on `vppsone.student_followups` (migration `20261011091243`), so the coordinator's lists and Student 360 read one history. There is no "sent" outcome and the sheet has no send button. Shown on Student 360's Family & contact card |
| D-266 | Student 360's support card lists every open support action and works it in place: Add update, Working on it, Done (with an optional "what was tried and what changed") | Notes §3/§6: one record in every place. The buttons call the notes board's own actions, which now also refresh `/students/[id]` and `/`, so finishing it on the profile clears it from the board and the teacher's Today. Done is offered only where `canWorkOnNote` allows. "Add support action" opens the note sheet, which shows an existing open action before a new one is added |
| D-267 | The teacher's roster shows three factual badges per child — attendance as days, papers not entered, next support review — instead of "Lowest: subject %"; teacher Today lists the support reviews the teacher owns by child's name | Notes §4: no ranking, no "weak". A paper counts as not entered only once its marking has begun in the class. A late review is amber, about the review rather than the child (`rosterBadges` in `teach/domain/teach.ts`) |
| D-268 | Attendance correction belongs to the class's current class teacher; Year Clear means the selected year's fees are fully cleared | Notes §10.3/§10.4, both already how the code works, recorded as the answers until the school says otherwise. `registerAuthority` takes `isOwner` from who holds the class today (`heldClassIds`), so after a reassignment the new class teacher corrects the class's registers and the old one stops; admins and coordinators keep `attendance:correct`. Every correction keeps its date, actor and history. Year Clear reads the selected session's obligation only; a family with nothing overdue but instalments still to come is not Year Clear (D-252) |
| D-269 | QA accounts can be shown a set school time on Today: `/login/test/clock?key=…&at=YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM` (empty `at` clears it) | The time-of-day timetable could only be checked live during school hours. The pretend time is read only for `@qa.vpps.local` accounts, only while the QA sign-in is on (not live, dev database, `QA_LOGIN_KEY` set), from an httpOnly cookie set behind the same key. It changes what Today shows (admin and teacher), never what anything saves |
| D-270 | Saving a payment never messages the parent; only a person pressing Send does | Found on the live check: the ₹100 test payment logged a WhatsApp receipt notice at save, nobody having pressed Send. The fee app's `whatsapp_receipt_notice_enabled` came across in the replica as `'true'`, and `sendReceiptNotice` honoured it. It failed only because no AiSensy key is configured here. The project rule is that nothing messages parents on its own, so `sendReceiptNotice` now refuses every trigger but `manual`, whatever the setting says, and the dev setting was set to `'false'` too. The Send button on a receipt is unchanged |
| D-271 | The desk drains the balance views before it reads back a saved receipt | The post only queues the materialized-view refresh and the fee action drains it in `after()`, so the desk's read-back ran first and the receipt printed the balance from before the payment (₹1,000 owed where ₹900 was right) until a reload. `savedResult` now awaits `drainFinancialViewRefresh()` before reading. It costs the cashier one refresh, but the paper in the parent's hand is the one figure a reload cannot correct |
| D-272 | The QA clock moves the greeting and the first-bell line too; a fee collector's Accounts page drops the close-day card | Found on the live check. Admin and teacher Today greeted by the real clock, and the first-bell line read "Period 1 running" at any hour; it now reads "Period 1 starts". A fee collector's /accounts offered "Count cash and close the day" that leads nowhere for the role and called them Accountant; without `fees:accounts` the card, the help step and the menu's day-close wording are gone and the page says Fee collector |
| D-273 | On a phone, Today's "Scheduled now" shows one period at a time (a Now/Next switch) and folds the list to five lessons — every cover change first — behind "Show all N classes"; a lesson with no cover change is one line. A desktop keeps Now and Next side by side in full | Raj, 12 Oct 2026: the timetable on the admin dashboard "is too long and huge on mobile view". With 22 classes the card was 916px at 390px wide — taller than the phone — because Now and Next stacked in full, each in a nested 340px scroll. It is 521px now. The "not a record of what was taught" line (D-258) stays, under the list on a phone. Fold rule is `foldedKeys()` in `schedule.ts`; `tests/mobile-ui/today-schedule.spec.ts` holds the height |

## Attendance corrections without approval — 2026-10-11

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-248 | No attendance correction waits for an admin, and a class teacher's own correction has no time limit. The 30-minute change window, the "Ask the office" request sheet, the approval queues (`/teach/corrections`, the coordinator's Changes tab, Needs-you correction items) and their counts are removed | Raj: class teachers fix their own register and need nobody's approval. The new teacher screen let a class teacher change a saved register for only 30 minutes after first saving it; after that she had to send a request for the office or coordinator to approve. That window was enforced in both server actions, not in the database, so no migration was needed. A class teacher now corrects whenever she needs to, choosing a reason in the save sheet. Raj chose removing the request flow over sending requests to the class teacher. The coordinator's weekly summary now counts corrections made rather than requests decided. A subject teacher's screen now says to tell the class teacher. The office still corrects any register. A correction still needs a reason and still makes a new revision (D-219), so the history says who changed what and why. Supersedes D-218's "anybody else in scope asks". `vppsone.attendance_corrections` and the `correction_request_id` path in `save_attendance_register` stay: migrations are additive, and the table keeps the old requests. Nothing writes to it any more. On dev, five requests were still pending when this shipped. They are left as history |

## Test-data smoke and responsiveness — 2026-10-05

| Decision | Outcome | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| D-247 | Native `fees:view` is checked by the shared fee-session adapter before any ported guard can return a session | The live role matrix passed, but runtime logs showed recovery RPC refusals from denied academic/exam requests. Next renders layouts and pages concurrently: the native fee layout redirected correctly while the ported read-only role still started fee queries and an `after` refresh. `getAuthenticatedStaff` now returns no fee session for excluded roles; `requireAuthenticatedStaff` checks native access before translating the role. Twenty-two regression cases cover all eight roles, both permission guards, signed-out requests and office payment refusal; seventeen fail on the prior implementation |
| D-240 | Run Vercel functions in Mumbai and read independent query chunks concurrently, bounded to four | Provider metadata confirmed both Supabase projects in `ap-south-1`, while the prior production deployment ran in `iad1`. Teacher Home's independent follow-up reads and directory chunks now overlap. Ordered results and errors are preserved, and a thrown request stops new work |
| D-241 | Navigation feedback represents all pending links, and idle pages run no progress animation | Reproduced false completion when idle links mounted, when one overlapping navigation finished, and when a URL committed with another link still pending. Link identities keep pending work separate; a new request cancels the previous dismissal timer. The URL fallback defers to pending links. Six regression cases cover this |
| D-242 | Attendance corrections and their Home counts belong to the selected academic session | A browser fixture reproduced a different session's correction appearing in the current queue. Both list and count now filter the register's session. The fixture deletes only its own two registers and their cascading correction rows |
| D-243 | Keep attendance row geometry fixed; verify the app origin before workflow fixtures; use bounded thread workers | Attendance press feedback respects reduced motion, and offscreen rows use existing content visibility. The workflow setup checks VPPS One's manifest before mutating test data. Vitest's Windows fork handshake timed out; the same 10,035 tests passed using two thread workers |
| D-244 | Screen smoke must resolve every dynamic route, follow internal streamed aliases, and keep QA cookies at their origin | `smoke:screens` supplies a cancelled duty and a finished zero-recipient campaign run on dev, with exact-ID cleanup verified in `finally`. Both strict roles and all-account screens run without silent fixture skips. The update-worker test uses a fresh cache version instead of an obsolete literal |
| D-245 | Workbook exports belong in the export log's kind labels; detect streamed errors before accepting HTTP 200 | The existing `students` and `round` enum values crashed the export log because their labels were missing. Both now have Hindi/English labels, with tests tied to their database migration. Screen smoke also reads Next error frames and visible error boundaries while ignoring unused messages inside scripts. The retired `/apps` bookmark is an alias to each account's Home, so its product expectation includes every staff role |
| D-246 | Refresh promise counters under the native fee permission, and keep background work alive | The legacy SQL map refuses the office role while the native fee lane grants it read access. An owned `vppsone` refresh checks `auth.uid()` and active `fees:view` before updating derived counters; anonymous execution is revoked and the public fee schema remains unchanged. Seventeen API assertions cover all QA accounts, the due-date boundary, repeat idempotency and exact fixture cleanup. The app captures its cookie client before `after` and returns the refresh promise |

## School branding and native navigation — 2026-10-05

The owner selected school blue/teal, branding plus native navigation first,
and one shared theme with familiar workflows. After reviewing the design, the
owner removed campus photos, retained logos/theme and authorised merging and
deploying the mobile/desktop PWA. `schoolBrand` owns the workspace identity, independently of receipt
configuration. CSS brand values deliberately differ from the fee reference;
Tailwind token names, geometry, fonts and financial semantics remain compatible.
School-workflow navigation stays inside VPPS One. Legacy Apps bookmarks and
saved start values resolve to authorised native destinations. No database target
or role grants change. See [asset sources](SCHOOL-BRANDING.md) and the
[next-stage parity checklist](NATIVE-INTEGRATION-CHECKLIST.md).

Settings, not facts to re-derive. Add a row rather than arguing a point twice.
An agent that picks a default the plan left open records it here.

| # | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| D-1 | VPPS One is a separate repo, Vercel project and Postgres schema from the fee app | The first attempt guarded the fee *module* but changed 45 shared platform files. A boundary enforced by structure cannot be forgotten; one enforced by review can |
| D-2 | No feature flags, no canary users, no release types, no approval gates | They existed only to protect `director@` from shared code. There is no shared code now |
| D-3 | Build on `schoolfees-dev` (`wtgxcptmucjerhufzjcf`) with test-session data; cut over to live at the end | Janmejay's call. Lets the whole app be built and used before any real data is involved |
| D-4 | Everything VPPS One owns lives in the `vppsone` schema | Makes the cutover additive-by-construction and collisions impossible |
| D-5 | Fee tables are SELECT-only, and no constraint points into `public` | A foreign key would be a write into the fee app's schema |
| D-6 | VPPS One has its own roles, not `public.users.role` | Being an accountant in the fee app says nothing about teaching |
| D-7 | Push to main through a PR the agent merges when CI is green | Keeps CI honest without adding a person-shaped wait |
| D-8 | Local folder is `Desktop\vppsone`, not `VPPS one` | npm refuses spaces and capitals in a project directory name |
| D-9 | Supabase Storage for files; Cloudflare only if storage or egress cost bites | One fewer service to run |
| D-10 | Parent-facing WhatsApp stays human-pressed | Unchanged from the original plan |
| D-11 | `office` may manage staff profiles, not just read them | PLAN §3 gives office the master data and PROGRESS 1.3 files staff under it. A profile is not an account — `user_id` is nullable and logins are created in Supabase Auth by an admin — so editing one cannot grant anybody access |
| D-12 | The root file is `src/proxy.ts`, not `middleware.ts` | Next.js 16 renamed the convention and deprecated the old name. The fee app is already on `proxy.ts`, so this matches what is running in production |
| D-13 | Writes have no RLS policy at all; server actions check the permission and then use the service-role client | Keeps "who may write" in one readable TypeScript file instead of spread across policies a role change would have to be kept in step with. The SELECT policy still governs every read |
| D-14 | Permission names are the whole platform's, not only Phase 1's | Six role lists written a phase at a time drift apart. Naming the full surface once makes the map readable as a whole, and `can()` is exhaustively tested against it |
| D-15 | `view_only` gets dashboards, not every `:view` | "Read-only dashboards" (PLAN §3) is oversight — the daily numbers and who teaches what. It stops short of per-child records: no notes, marks, documents or collection |
| D-16 | An unknown role resolves to `view_only`, never to anything higher | A role added to the `staff_role` enum before the code that understands it must not be able to arrive as an admin |
| D-17 | `short_name` immutability is a database trigger, not only a form rule | It is the timetable's join key; a rename orphans every slot that named it. A server action can be rewritten, a trigger cannot be forgotten |
| D-18 | `short_name` is unique case-insensitively | "RKS" and "rks" are the same teacher to everyone except a plain unique constraint, and a case-variant duplicate is a silently wrong timetable import |
| D-19 | Exposing the `vppsone` schema to PostgREST is a migration file, applied additively | Creating the schema is not enough — PostgREST answered `PGRST106 Invalid schema` until it was exposed.
